### [1] [Assessing the Downstream Utility of Evidence-Aware Retrieval in RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26379)
*Utshab Kumar Ghosh,Debayan Mukhopadhyay,Shubham Chatterjee*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文研究RAG检索评估中"答案支持信号"（answer-support signal）在下游任务中的价值，发现该信号虽能改变检索排序，但其下游效益并不统一：对检索器训练无稳定提升，对系统选择取决于生成器指令，对答案质量预测不稳健，在直接证据干预中不同评估器结论不一。作者主张RAG评估方法应依其目标用途进行分类评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检索增强生成（RAG）的检索评估正从单纯的"主题相关性"转向"是否能提供支持生成的证据"。论文想探究这种与下游证据需求的对齐，是否真的让检索评估对基于它构建的决策更有用——即评估信号的改进是否必然带来下游效益的提升。

Method: 在五个检索基准和一个端到端TREC RAG 2025设置中，将"答案支持信号"应用于四种角色：(1) 比较不同检索器；(2) 指导检索器训练与系统选择；(3) 预测下游答案质量；(4) 过滤提供给生成器的证据。通过系统性对比实验评估该信号在各角色中的实际价值。

Result: 答案支持信号改变了检索排名，但下游价值不一致：对检索器训练无可靠改进；用于系统选择时取决于生成器如何使用证据；基于该信号的检索分数对未见主题的答案质量预测不稳健；在直接证据干预中，人类标注确认过滤保留了有用证据，但不同答案评估器对答案是否提升结论不一致。

Conclusion: 使检索评估更贴近生成所需的证据，并不自动使该评估的每个下游用途更可靠。RAG评估方法应针对其具体支持的比较、决策和结论来进行评估，即评估方法的价值是上下文相关的。

Abstract: Retrieval evaluation for retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) is increasingly designed around whether retrieved passages contain evidence that can support generation, rather than topical relevance alone. We study whether this closer alignment with downstream evidence needs also makes retrieval evaluation more useful for the decisions built from it.
  Across five retrieval benchmarks and an end-to-end TREC RAG 2025 setting, we examine an answer-support signal in four roles: comparing retrievers, guiding retrieval training and system selection, predicting downstream answer quality, and filtering the evidence supplied to a generator. The signal changes retrieval rankings, but its downstream value is not uniform. It does not reliably improve retriever training; the benefit of using it for system selection depends on how the generator is instructed to use the retrieved evidence; and retrieval scores based on it do not robustly predict answer quality on unseen topics. In a direct evidence intervention, human annotators confirm that filtering preferentially preserves passages containing useful answer evidence, yet different answer evaluators reach different conclusions about whether the resulting answers improve.
  These results show that making retrieval evaluation more closely reflect the evidence needed for generation does not by itself make every downstream use of that evaluation more reliable. RAG evaluation methods should therefore be assessed with respect to the particular comparisons, decisions, and conclusions they are intended to support.

</details>


### [2] [Preference Flow Matching with Spectral Factorization for Micro-video Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26579)
*Xinxin Dong,Haokai Ma,Fei Hu,YuZe Zheng,Bin Wu,Yonghui Yang,Xiaodong Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出PrismRec框架，通过频谱分解将微观视频表示拆分为静态语义与动态因素，并以校准后的上下文作为结构化条件引导流匹配生成过程，在四个数据集上超越SOTA最多22.65%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有微观视频推荐方法存在两个问题：一是将帧序列压缩成单一整体表征，混淆了稳定的视觉语义与演进的动态信息；二是基于扩散/流匹配的推荐器仅以粗粒度行为上下文作为生成条件，忽略了内部时间结构对偏好形成的影响。

Method: PrismRec包含两个核心模块：(1) Spectral Semantic Factorization (SSF)，类比棱镜分光，通过先验引导的可学习频率掩码在时频域中将帧级表示分解为互补的静态语义因子与动态因子；(2) Context-Calibrated Preference Matching (CPM)，根据每个用户的具体敏感性对各因子加权，并将校准后的上下文作为结构化条件注入匹配轨迹，使视频内容成为偏好形成的内在驱动力。

Result: 在两个平台的四个数据集上，PrismRec相比SOTA基线最高提升22.65%，同时在所有对比方法中拥有最低的推理成本和峰值内存占用。

Conclusion: 将视频内容解耦为静态语义与动态因素、并以用户校准上下文引导流匹配生成过程，能有效提升微观视频推荐的性能与效率，验证了内容作为偏好形成内在驱动力的有效性。

Abstract: Micro-video recommendation aims to infer user preferences from historical interactions and multimodal video content, thereby identifying the next video of interest. However, prevailing methods compress frame sequences into a single holistic representation, entangling the stable visual semantics and the evolving dynamics that jointly shape user preferences. Meanwhile, diffusion- and flow matching-based recommenders condition their generation process solely on coarse behavioral context, leaving its internal temporal structure outside preference formation. We therefore propose PrismRec, a Preference Flow Matching framework with Spectral Factorization for Micro-video Recommendation. Analogous to a prism that disperses white light into its constituent spectrum, PrismRec devises Spectral Semantic Factorization (SSF) to derive complementary static semantic and dynamic factors from frame-level representations via a prior-guided learnable frequency mask in the temporal frequency domain. Then, it proposes Context-Calibrated Preference Matching (CPM) to weigh them with each user's specific sensitivity and inject the calibrated context as a structured condition to steer the matching trajectory toward the target representation, making video content as an intrinsic driver of preference formation rather than auxiliary side information. Experiments on four datasets from two platforms show that PrismRec surpasses the SOTA baseline by up to 22.65%, with the lowest inference cost and peak memory among the compared methods.

</details>


### [3] [hoBIT: A Profile-Aware Retrieval-Augmented Chatbot for University Academic Advising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26604)
*Yoonseo Kim,Seongmin Lee,Joongheon Kim,SeongKu Kang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: proFILL 将基于规则的大学学术咨询聊天机器人 hoBIT 改造为具档案感知的检索增强生成（RAG）系统，通过渐进式获取查询所需的用户档案属性（如院系、入学批次、学位项目）来条件化检索，避免档案盲检索带来的不适用证据问题，并在实验中优于多种 RAG 基线且被目标用户偏好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大学学术咨询场景中，相同问题可能因用户的院系、入学批次和学位项目不同而需要不同答案，导致档案盲（profile-blind）的检索器会返回看似合理但实际不适用（plausible but inapplicable）的证据。

Method: proFILL 将 hoBIT（现有的基于规则咨询聊天机器人）转化为档案感知的 RAG 系统。其核心策略是：不要求用户一开始就提供完整的用户档案，而是依据查询意图和初始检索到的证据，渐进式地只获取每个查询实际需要的档案属性，并利用这些属性对档案感知索引进行条件化检索。

Result: 广泛的实验和人类偏好研究表明，proFILL 优于多种 RAG 基线，被目标用户更偏好，并且在开放权重模型上依然有效，支持成本高效的本地部署。

Conclusion: proFILL 证明了通过渐进式档案获取与条件化检索，可以在不多次打搅用户的前提下显著提升学术咨询 RAG 系统的回答准确性与适用性，且兼容开放权重模型以支持实际部署。

Abstract: In university academic advising, identical questions can require different answers depending on a student's department, admission cohort, and degree program, causing profile-blind retrievers to surface plausible but inapplicable evidence. We present proFILL, a method for transforming hoBIT, our college's current rule-based advising chatbot, into a profile-aware retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) system. Rather than requiring a complete user profile upfront, proFILL progressively acquires only the profile attributes needed for each query, guided by both the query intent and the initially retrieved evidence, and uses them to condition retrieval over a profile-aware index. Extensive experiments and a human preference study show that proFILL outperforms diverse RAG baselines, is preferred by target users, and remains effective with open-weight models for cost-effective on-premise deployment.

</details>


### [4] [When Does Supervised Fine-Tuning Reduce Instruction Sensitivity?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26661)
*Jaekeol Choi*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文研究了监督微调（SFT）如何改变大语言模型对任务指令表述变化的敏感性。通过Qwen3（1.7B/4B/8B）、Mistral-7B和Gemma-2-9B在MS MARCO和ESCI-English上的受控实验发现：SFT并不统一降低指令敏感性，其效果取决于模型规模、适应设置，且测量到的敏感度还受预测与评分协议的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型对同一任务不同措辞的指令会表现出显著的性能差异，但传统的任务特定监督微调（SFT）如何改变这种指令敏感性仍不清楚。理解这一点对于可靠评估模型鲁棒性和改进微调策略至关重要。

Method: 在固定模型检查点上使用多种释义指令评估性能，将指令敏感性定义为任务性能的标准差。进行受控规模分析（Qwen3 1.7B/4B/8B在MS MARCO上），辅以跨族系检查（Mistral-7B和Gemma-2-9B），并采用查询级bootstrap分析、配对对比和三种随机种子验证，同时在ESCI-English上对比自由生成与基于似然的强制选择两种评估协议。

Result: SFT前，指令敏感性随Qwen3模型规模增大而急剧下降。1.7B和4B上SFT一致降低敏感性约54-71%；8B上个体敏感性变化统计上不显著，但训练指令间的配对对比在bootstrap分析下统计可靠且跨三种随机种子方向一致。Gemma-2-9B与Qwen3-8B方向一致，而Mistral-7B不然。ESCI-English实验显示自由生成与似然强制选择评估可得出定性不同的鲁棒性结论。

Conclusion: SFT并不统一降低指令敏感性，其鲁棒性效果取决于适应设置和模型规模；同时，测量到的指令敏感性本质上依赖于预测与评分协议的选择。这提示LLM鲁棒性评估需要更精细、多协议的方法。

Abstract: Large language models can exhibit substantial performance variation across alternative formulations of the same task instruction, yet it remains unclear how conventional task-specific supervised fine-tuning (SFT) changes this instruction sensitivity. We study this question by evaluating fixed model checkpoints under multiple paraphrased instructions and defining instruction sensitivity as the standard deviation of task performance across them. We conduct a controlled scale analysis with Qwen3 models at 1.7B, 4B, and 8B on MS MARCO, together with targeted cross-family checks using Mistral-7B and Gemma-2-9B. Before SFT, instruction sensitivity decreases sharply with Qwen3 model scale. At 1.7B and 4B, SFT consistently reduces sensitivity across training instructions, with reductions of approximately 54--71%. At 8B, individual sensitivity changes are not statistically distinguishable from zero, but paired contrasts between training instructions are statistically reliable under query-level bootstrap analysis and have consistent directions across all three random seeds. Gemma-2-9B shows the same directional training-instruction contrast as Qwen3-8B, whereas Mistral-7B does not, suggesting that the strength of this effect also varies across models. Experiments on ESCI-English further show that free-generation and likelihood-based forced-choice evaluation can yield qualitatively different robustness conclusions even when valid-label generation is nearly perfect and average task performance is similar. Overall, SFT does not uniformly reduce instruction sensitivity: its robustness effect depends on the adaptation setting, while measured sensitivity can additionally depend on the prediction and scoring protocol.

</details>


### [5] [BLANC: Discovering Patent White Space via Changes in Normalized Pointwise Mutual Information Between Multi-View Clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26685)
*Shuichi Miyazawa,Kensuke Fujii*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出BLANC管道，通过多视角神经主题建模与NPMI条件化检测，系统性识别专利空白领域（已知全局但局部未探索的组合），并在人工耗竭实验与实际专利案例中验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专利空白领域的识别对战略研发规划至关重要，但现有方法依赖人工专利映射或单一视角聚类，缺乏量化间隙检测手段，无法系统、客观地发现未开发的潜在价值区域。

Method: 三阶段管道：(1) 沿应用/用途、新颖性、创造性三个语义维度进行多视角神经主题建模；(2) 用归一化逐点互信息（NPMI）量化跨维度聚类关联；(3) 条件检测，当语料被用户指定关键词过滤后NPMI显著下降的组合被标记，通过新指标ΔNPMI表征"全局已确立、局部未探索"的空白区域。

Result: 在ML/AI（G06N，5417件）与玻璃组分（C03C，1982件）两个USPTO公开语料上，人工耗竭目标组合75%文档后，BLANC分别恢复34.1%与27.3%的耗竭组合；191次诱饵试验中目标从未被恢复。单视角合并零恢复，传统共生度量在随机移除下也标记目标、缺乏特异性。专有案例中关键词"氟"揭示的氟表面处理×翘曲抑制候选组合（ΔNPMI高达0.48）被专家独立确认。

Conclusion: BLANC通过多视角分解与ΔNPMI条件化检测，实现了对专利空白领域的定量、可解释且高特异性识别，优于基于普通共生或单视角的方法，并能在真实场景中捕捉到专家认可的潜在创新组合。

Abstract: Identifying white space --- the unexplored but potentially valuable regions of a patent landscape --- is essential for strategic R&D planning, yet existing methods rely on manual patent mapping or apply single-view clustering without quantitative gap detection. We propose BLANC (Blank Landscape Analysis through NPMI Conditioning), a three-phase pipeline combining (1) multi-view neural topic modeling along three semantic dimensions (application/use, novelty, inventive step); (2) Normalized Pointwise Mutual Information (NPMI) to quantify cross-dimensional cluster association; and (3) conditional detection that flags combinations whose NPMI drops when the corpus is filtered by a user-specified keyword. The drop is captured by a new metric, $Δ$NPMI, which identifies combinations "established globally, unexplored locally." Because white space has no ground truth, we evaluate BLANC on two public USPTO corpora --- machine learning/AI (5,417 patents, CPC G06N) and glass compositions (1,982 patents, CPC C03C) --- by artificially depleting known technology combinations and testing recovery. When three-quarters of a target pair's documents are removed, BLANC recovers 34.1% (ML/AI) and 27.3% (glass) of the depleted combinations, whereas size-matched removals not aimed at them (random documents, or those of a different established combination) essentially never do: the target is never recovered in 191 decoy trials. Collapsing the three semantic views into one recovers nothing, while prior co-occurrence measures also flag the target under random removal, offering no specificity. In a proprietary case (302 float glass / glass-ceramics patents), the keyword "fluorine" reveals a fluorine surface treatment $\times$ warpage suppression candidate ($Δ$NPMI up to 0.48) that experts had independently identified.

</details>


### [6] [Beyond a Single Story: Meta-Reviewing Sparse and Incomplete User-generated Contents for Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26728)
*Hongren Wang,Tianjun Wei,Yingpeng Du,Jie Zhang,Yin-Leng Theng*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: MOSAIC通过聚合邻居用户的评论生成元评论（meta-review），利用MMoE架构联合优化评分预测和属性情感预测，缓解推荐系统中用户生成内容的数据稀疏和不完整问题，在四个真实数据集上取得了优于现有方法的准确率和可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推荐系统中依赖用户生成内容（如文本评论）的方法面临两个独特挑战：缺失评论（交互缺乏评论）和不完整评论（评论只覆盖部分属性）。现有方法往往忽视这些问题，导致推荐准确性下降。作者受学术同行评审中元评论（meta-review）概念的启发，希望找到一种方式在UGC稀疏和不完整的情况下仍能有效建模用户偏好。

Method: MOSAIC的方法包括三个核心组件：(1) 为每个目标用户构建元评论，通过聚合邻居用户评论中的属性情感证据来实现；(2) 采用多门控混合专家（MMoE）架构联合优化评分预测任务和元评论属性情感预测任务；(3) 使用注意力模块将聚合的元评论信号个性化适配到每个目标用户，从而同时生成精炼的评分预测和属性级别的解释。

Result: 在四个真实世界数据集上的实验表明，MOSAIC在推荐准确率和解释质量两方面均一致性地优于最先进的基线方法，有效缓解了UGC稀疏性和不完整性问题，并且对于交互历史有限的用户也能带来一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: MOSAIC通过元评论聚合和MMoE多任务学习，提供了一种有效应对UGC数据稀疏和不完整问题的推荐方案，同时兼顾了推荐准确性和属性级可解释性，尤其对冷启动用户表现出稳定的增益。

Abstract: Data sparsity remains a long-standing challenge in recommender systems, and it becomes more severe for methods relying on user-generated content (UGC) such as textual reviews, which capture fine-grained preferences but require more user efforts to produce. As a result, UGC exhibits (1) missing reviews, where interactions lack any review, and (2) incomplete reviews, where available reviews cover only a subset of relevant attributes. Existing approaches often overlook these UGC-specific issues, leading to degraded accuracy. Motivated by meta-review in academic peer review, we propose MOSAIC (Meta-review On Sparse And Incomplete user-generated Content), which constructs a meta-review for each target user by aggregating attribute-sentiment evidence from neighbor users' reviews. A multi-gate mixture-of-experts (MMoE) architecture jointly optimizes rating prediction and meta-review attribute-sentiment prediction, while an attention module personalizes the aggregated meta-review signals to each target user, yielding both refined rating predictions and attribute-level explanations. Experiments on four real-world datasets demonstrate that MOSAIC consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines in both recommendation accuracy and explanation quality, mitigating UGC sparsity and incompleteness while delivering consistent gains for users with limited interaction history.

</details>


### [7] [STREAM: An Objective-Driven and Uncertainty-Aware Framework for Industrial Energy Data Acquisition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26754)
*Zhipeng Ma,Bo Nørregaard Jørgensen,Zheng Grace Ma*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出STREAM框架，一个目标驱动且考虑不确定性的工业能源数据采集工作流，涵盖目标规范、技术要求、资源映射、源提取、归档元数据和数据库迁移六个阶段，并通过两个工业批处理案例验证了数据可访问性不等同于分析适用性的核心观点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业能源管理需要将能源使用与设备状态、生产批次、物料流和工艺条件关联的数据集，但传统采集流程往往只强调连通性和存储，未能验证可用信号是否满足既定能源性能评估的需求，缺乏目标到数据的可追溯性。

Method: STREAM框架包含六个阶段：目标规范、技术要求、资源映射、源提取、归档元数据和数据库迁移。相比原始概念序列，新增了阶段级工件、最小证据门控、源适用性规则、元数据模板、不确定性评估量规和案例特定可追溯性矩阵。不确定性评估涵盖测量、时间、上下文和处理四个维度，贯穿所有六个阶段。

Result: 通过两个工业批处理案例（铸造厂感应炉熔炼和奶酪粉干燥，基于SCADA和生产订单数据）验证，结果表明数据可访问性并不等同于分析适用性，STREAM能够支持关于即时数据使用、分析限制和优先基础设施改进的透明决策。

Conclusion: STREAM框架通过端到端的目标到数据可追溯性输出，为工业能源数据采集提供了系统化方法，使决策者能清晰区分可直接使用的数据与需限制分析或需优先改进基础设施的数据场景。

Abstract: Industrial energy management requires datasets that connect energy use with equipment states, production batches, material flows, and process conditions. However, conventional acquisition workflows commonly emphasize connectivity and storage without verifying whether accessible signals satisfy the requirements of a defined energy-performance assessment. This paper presents STREAM, an objective-driven and uncertainty-aware framework comprising Specification of Objectives, Technical Requirements, Resource Mapping, Extraction from Sources, Archival Metadata, and Migration to Database. STREAM is the central workflow: objective-to-data traceability is its end-to-end output, while measurement, temporal, contextual, and processing uncertainty are assessed across all six stages. Compared with the original conceptual STREAM sequence, this paper adds stage-level artifacts, minimum-evidence gates, source-suitability rules, a metadata template, an uncertainty rubric, and case-specific traceability matrices. The framework is validated through two industrial batch-process cases: induction-furnace melting in a foundry and cheese-powder drying using SCADA and production-order data. The results demonstrate that data accessibility is not equivalent to analytical suitability and show how STREAM supports transparent decisions about immediate data use, analytical restrictions, and prioritized infrastructure improvements.

</details>


### [8] [When Memory Takes Gradients: Collaborative Vector Memory for Agentic Recommender Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26895)
*Hanchong Chen,Xing Tang,Lingjie Li,Xiongfeng Shan,Xiuqiang He*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出CoVeMem（协作向量记忆），将智能体推荐系统中基于文本的用户记忆向量化为协作向量（由冻结的LightGCN状态构成），使LLM通过软token读取历史交互状态来进行推荐决策，在四个基准的20个指标单元中19个达到或超过最强的基于文本记忆的协作智能体，且无需额外LLM调用维护记忆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体推荐系统用文本记忆（LLM生成的自然语言叙述）记录用户，但存在两个限制：一是文本一次只能重写一条，利用完整交互历史代价过高；二是协作证据（对全目录的梯度相似度）无法在翻译成句子的过程中保留。因此需要一种能向量化协作核心的记忆形式。

Method: CoVeMem将智能体记忆的协作核心向量化：冻结的LightGCN用户态和物品态构成记忆库；每次决策时，候选集自身检索最相关的历史状态，以软token形式与轻量文本画像一起进入LLM上下文；通过对比对齐到物品语义锚点，再与掩码候选进行列表级协同训练，使模型学会读取这些状态并通过其排序；用逐点的yes/no读出为每个候选打分。

Result: 在四个指令驱动的推荐基准上，CoVeMem在20个指标单元中的19个达到或超过最强的协作文本记忆智能体，且记忆维护零额外LLM调用（仅需共享的静态画像），而文本记忆每交互一次需调用一次LLM。

Conclusion: 协作向量记忆使完整交互历史不再遥不可及，记忆可以接受梯度训练，成为智能体可学习的参数——既决定记住什么，也决定如何读取所记住的内容，为推荐智能体的记忆机制提供了新范式。

Abstract: Agentic recommender systems ground each decision of a large language model (LLM) in a persistent memory of the user, and in existing agents that memory is text: a narrative written and maintained by further LLM calls. Text limits this memory in two ways. It is updated one rewrite at a time, so exploiting the full interaction history is prohibitively expensive; and collaborative evidence, graded similarity over an entire catalog, does not survive translation into sentences. We propose CoVeMem (Collaborative Vector Memory), which vectorizes the collaborative core of the agent's memory. Frozen LightGCN user and item states form the memory bank; at each decision, the candidate set itself retrieves the most relevant historical states, which enter the LLM's context as soft tokens alongside a light textual profile. Contrastive alignment to item-semantic anchors, followed by listwise co-training with masked candidates, teaches the model to read these states and to rank through them; a pointwise yes/no readout scores each candidate. Across four instruction-grounded recommendation benchmarks, CoVeMem matches or exceeds the strongest collaborative text-memory agent on 19 of 20 metric cells while requiring zero additional LLM calls for memory maintenance beyond the shared static profile, against per-interaction calls for text memory. The memory now takes gradients: the full interaction history, out of reach for text, becomes available as training data for what the agent remembers and for how it reads what it remembers.

</details>


### [9] [Topology-Masked Unified Backbone for Joint Feature Interaction and Multi-Domain Sequence Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27005)
*Zhihao Zhu,Dezheng Han,Jikang Xia,Shuaishuai Guo*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出MaskRec，一种基于拓扑掩码的统一token交互架构，将异构特征交互与多域行为序列建模统一到同一注意力空间中进行工业CVR预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工业排序模型通常将特征交互和多域用户行为序列建模拆分为独立模块；虽有一体化框架的尝试，但往往依赖模块间协调，未能将所有信息源充分组织在同一交互空间中。

Method: MaskRec将异构特征、多域行为序列和上下文信号统一为token表示，引入可学习的全局记忆token和域级记忆token作为信息聚合节点；提出结构化注意力掩码TopoMask，根据不同信息源的结构差异和建模需求选择性地允许或阻断注意力连接；并设计了双路径交互查询生成模块，在统一骨干网络之前注入候选条件化的用户-物品交互信号。

Result: 在腾讯广告算法竞赛数据集上进行实验，MaskRec相比官方基线取得了稳定的性能提升，验证了该统一框架在工业CVR预测中的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过拓扑约束的注意力机制，MaskRec在统一token空间中同时完成异构特征交互与多域序列建模，为工业级CVR预测提供了一种更高效、结构化的统一建模方案。

Abstract: Large-scale post-click conversion rate (CVR) prediction requires jointly modeling heterogeneous feature interactions and dependencies over multi-domain user behavior sequences. Existing industrial ranking models usually handle these two aspects with separate modules. Recent unified architectures attempt to incorporate them into a single framework, but such unification often relies on coordination between modules and does not fully organize all information sources within the same interaction space. To address this problem, we propose MaskRec, a topology-masked unified token interaction architecture for feature interaction and multi-domain sequence modeling. MaskRec transforms heterogeneous features, multi-domain behavior sequences, and contextual signals into unified token representations, and further introduces learnable global memory tokens and domain-level memory tokens as information aggregation nodes. Based on this unified token space, MaskRec designs a structured attention mask, TopoMask, which selectively enables or blocks attention connections according to the structural differences and modeling requirements of different information sources. In this way, heterogeneous feature interaction and multi-domain sequence modeling are performed within the same topology-constrained attention process. In addition, MaskRec incorporates a dual-path interactive query generation module to inject candidate-conditioned user--item interaction signals before the unified backbone. Experiments on the Tencent Advertising Algorithm Competition dataset show that MaskRec achieves stable improvements over the official baseline, validating the effectiveness of the proposed unified framework for industrial CVR prediction.

</details>


### [10] [Conversational Recommendation over Live E-Commerce Catalogues with Self-Refreshing Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27006)
*Ante Kapetanovic,Tomislav Duricic,Dionizije Fa,Andro Mercep,Emanuel Lacic*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个基于大语言模型的多轮对话购物助手系统，其核心创新在于能够处理实时变化的电商商品目录。系统通过自刷新检索器对商品feed进行增量同步（基于哈希检测新增、变更、删除），LLM仅用于意图分类和偏好搜集，检索、重排和多样性选择均由专用函数执行。演示系统以WhatsApp购物助手的形态呈现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的对话式推荐系统通常在静态、预索引的商品集合上进行评估，但电商目录是动态变化的——商品会被添加、移除、改价、补货。静态索引无法反映实时目录变化，导致推荐结果滞后或失效，需要一种能够与实时商品目录同步的对话式推荐系统。

Method: 系统包含三个核心部分：(1) 自刷新检索器：接收商家产品feed，丰富记录并同步到向量索引，通过每个商品项的哈希值识别新增、变更、删除和未变化，仅处理增量而非全量重建；(2) 控制器驱动的对话层：基于上述索引，LLM仅用于意图分类和偏好获取，而检索、重排和多样性选择由专用函数完成；(3) WhatsApp演示集成：目录变更在下次成功同步后即可反映到推荐结果中。

Result: 系统以WhatsApp购物助手形式展示，实现实时目录变更（商品新增、下架、改价等）在下次同步后反映到推荐结果中的能力。相关资源（实时聊天机器人、文档和录屏演示）已在GitHub上公开。

Conclusion: 该工作提出了一种商家无关的多轮对话购物助手，解决了LLM对话推荐系统在实时动态目录场景下的适配问题，通过增量同步机制与LLM+专用函数的分工架构，实现了高效且可扩展的实时对话式购物推荐。

Abstract: Conversational recommender systems based on large language models (LLMs) are usually evaluated on static, pre-indexed item collections, yet e-commerce catalogues change continuously as products are added or removed, repriced, and restocked. We present a merchant-agnostic, multi-turn conversational shopping assistant that operates over such live catalogues. Its central component is a self-refreshing retriever that ingests a merchant product feed, enriches the records, and synchronizes them into a vector index. On each run, per-item hashes identify which products are new, changed, deleted, or unchanged, so only the delta is processed rather than rebuilding the whole catalogue. A controller-based dialogue layer consumes this index, using an LLM only for intent classification and preference elicitation while retrieval, reranking, and diversity selection run as dedicated functions. Our demonstration is a WhatsApp shopping assistant in which catalogue changes reach the recommendations after the next successful sync. A live chatbot, documentation, and a recorded walkthrough are available at https://github.com/infobip/infobip-agentic-crs.

</details>


### [11] [ProRetrieval: Learning to Orchestrate Hybrid Search via Executable Program Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27017)
*Chengsong You,Zhen Sun,Yunhai Hu,Junwei Zhou,Xiaoyu Cao,Binyu Li,Ziyan Zhao,Weiyao Wang,Liren Lu,Zhijie Ye,Yumo Cao,Yitao Long,Yiwei Xu,Qiyi Jiang,Xuanyi Fu,Yufan Chen,Yilun Li,Rongkang Xiong,Yiran Zou,Nan Du*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: ProRetrieval 将语言模型重新定位为检索编排器，通过一个混合DSL（交织SQL运算符和向量检索原语）为给定查询合成可执行程序，用SQL逻辑代数为异构候选集提供融合，并在4B参数模型上通过GRPO/DAPO训练超越GPT-5.5和Claude Opus 4.7等强大基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界的检索任务通常需要将结构化约束与文本/图像上的语义意图通过任意布尔逻辑组合。现有混合检索管道（如倒数排名融合或自查询检索器）只支持固定形式的组合，而近期基于强化学习的检索器仅将语言模型训练为单一后端的查询生成器，异构检索路径的编排超出了其动作空间。

Method: ProRetrieval 将语言模型重塑为检索编排器：输入自然语言查询后，模型合成一个混合DSL中的可执行程序，该DSL在结构化字段上交织SQL运算符，并在文本和图像上使用向量检索原语，SQL本身提供融合异构候选集的逻辑代数。模型使用Qwen3-4B，采用GRPO和DAPO进行训练，配以分层四项奖励函数，并在两个新构建的Amazon产品与Enron邮件基准上评估。

Result: 4B参数的ProRetrieval在电商任务上Hit@1达0.81（对比GPT-5.5的0.69），在邮件任务上达0.91（对比0.86），优于GPT-5.5、Claude Opus 4.7以及一系列检索、LLM增强、结构化查询和图基线方法。

Conclusion: ProRetrieval通过将程序合成与混合检索相结合，使语言模型能够灵活编排异构检索路径，在复杂组合检索任务上显著超越现有最强模型和基线，展示了以SQL作为统一融合代数的有效性。

Abstract: Real-world retrieval often composes structured constraints with semantic intents over text and images through arbitrary Boolean logic. Existing hybrid pipelines such as reciprocal rank fusion or self-querying retrievers admit only a fixed form of composition, while recent reinforcement-learning retrievers train the language model as a query generator for a single backend, leaving the orchestration of heterogeneous retrieval paths outside its action space. We propose ProRetrieval, which recasts the language model as a retrieval orchestrator: given a natural-language query, it synthesizes an executable program in a hybrid DSL interleaving SQL operators over structured fields with vector-retrieval primitives over text and images, with SQL itself providing the logical algebra that fuses heterogeneous candidate sets. We train Qwen3-4B with GRPO and DAPO under a hierarchical four-term reward, and evaluate on two new benchmarks built from Amazon products and Enron email. Our 4B model surpasses GPT-5.5 (Hit@1 0.81 vs. 0.69 on e-commerce; 0.91 vs. 0.86 on email) and Claude Opus 4.7 and a comprehensive suite of retrieval, LLM-augmented, structured-query, and graph-based baselines. Code: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/ProRetrieval/; data: https://huggingface.co/datasets/anonymous-7219/ProRetrieval.

</details>


### [12] [Astar: Learning to Propose Evolution Directions for Self-Evolving Industrial AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27287)
*Jinxin Hu,Hao Deng,Haibo Xing,Lingyu Mu,Muyu Zou,Weiqin Yang,Sirui Chen,Bohao Wang,Zhezheng Hao,Hao Zhang,Zulong Chen,Shizhun Wang,Yu Zhang,Xiaoyi Zeng,Jiawei Chen*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出Astar，一种基于训练的方法，从工业系统的海量迭代历史中学习专门的"进化引导"模型，以取代人类专家在AI迭代循环中提出进化方向的关键角色。Astar在真实部署中单次提案成功率远超人类专家和通用大模型，并成功实现了全自动闭环迭代。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代AI系统的迭代循环中，实现、训练和评估三个环节已日益自动化，但提出有效进化方向这一起点仍严重依赖高级专家。通用大模型（包括GPT-5.5）只能提供泛泛且不贴合的提议，因为所需专业知识是通过经验积累而非显式编码的，难以直接注入。因此需要一种能从工业系统迭代历史中学习专门进化引导能力的方案。

Method: 针对四个挑战（稀疏监督、噪声数据、巨大方向空间、昂贵的验证），从数据和模型两个层面解决。数据侧：设计管线将有噪声的历史提交转变为大规模干净进化语料库，通过成对样本扩增和噪声过滤实现。模型侧：通过mid-training、SFT和RL三阶段训练模型，用分层提示（hierarchical hints）引导进化方向生成，并在RL中使用奖励模型作为快速替代评估器。

Result: Astar已部署在阿里巴巴Lazada广告系统中。Astar-8B在真实执行评估中单次提案成功率达0.6786，远超人类专家（0.3229）和最强通用大模型（0.3071）。此外，Astar在两周内引导了20次连续迭代，将离线Hitrate@200提升23.6%，在线A/B测试中GMV相对提升4.86%，广告收入相对提升1.82%。

Conclusion: Astar证明了AI可以接管进化方向提出这一关键角色，填补了AI系统自动迭代循环中最后的人工瓶颈，实现全自动迭代闭环。基于训练的方法相比直接使用通用LLM，能更有效地从工业迭代历史中捕获难以显式表达的专家经验知识。

Abstract: Modern AI systems advance through continuous iteration: a loop of proposing evolution directions, implementing code, training, and evaluation. While the latter three stages are increasingly automated, the starting point --- proposing effective evolution directions --- remains a critical bottleneck that still relies heavily on senior experts. In this work, we explore whether AI can take over this role. We find that general-purpose LLMs, even the advanced GPT-5.5, offer only generic and misaligned suggestions: the required expertise is accumulated through experience rather than explicitly codified, and thus hard to inject directly.
  To this end, we propose Astar, a training-based approach that learns a specialized evolution-guiding model from the abundant iteration histories of industrial systems. Realizing this idea, however, raises four challenges: sparse supervision, noisy data, a vast direction space, and prohibitively expensive verification. We address them along two fronts. On the data side, we design a pipeline that turns noisy historical commits into a large, clean evolutionary corpus via pairwise sample expansion and noise filtering. On the model side, we train the model through mid-training, SFT, and RL, guiding evolution direction generation with hierarchical hints and using the reward model in RL as a fast surrogate evaluator.
  Astar has been deployed in Alibaba's Lazada advertising system for evolution direction proposal. Astar-8B achieves a single-proposal success rate of 0.6786 in real-execution evaluation, far exceeding human experts (0.3229) and the strongest general-purpose LLM (0.3071). More importantly, Astar closes the loop and enables fully automatic iteration: it guided 20 consecutive iterations over two weeks, improving offline Hitrate@200 by 23.6%, while an online A/B test yielded relative lifts of 4.86% in GMV and 1.82% in advertising revenue.

</details>

*Darpan Singhal,Matan Mandelbrod,Tal Franji,Manasa Kolla,Vipul Gaba,Yuri Brovman*

TL;DR: 本文介绍了eBay在商品详情页（View-Item页面）上展示附加信号信息的系统，采用两阶段XGBoost模型来最优地选择和填充信号内容，实验结果表明整体GMB提升0.08%，配件类目GMB提升0.58%。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: eBay商品详情页上的信号（短文本或视觉片段）能为用户提供与所查看商品相关的额外上下文信息，目的是促进智能购买并激励用户参与。如何最优地选择和填充这些信号以最大化商业价值是一个关键问题。

Method: 提出一个两阶段基于XGBoost的模型：第一阶段负责信号的选择/过滤，第二阶段负责信号的排序与优化布局，从而在VI页面上最优地填充信号内容。

Result: 在线实验显示该方法在整体GMB（总购买商品额）上带来0.08%的提升，在配件及附件类目中GMB提升0.58%，主要归因于高平均价格商品的转化率提升。

Conclusion: 两阶段XGBoost模型能够有效优化eBay商品详情页的信号填充策略，带来显著的商业收益，特别是在高单价商品和配件类目中效果更为突出。

Abstract: Signals are short textual or visual snippets displayed on the eBay View-Item (VI) page, providing additional, contextual information for users about the viewed item. The aim of displaying these signals is to facilitate intelligent purchase and to incentivize engagement. In this paper, we present a 2 stage xgboost based model that optimally populates the VI page with signals. This approach has shown a 0.08% lift in overall GMB (Gross Merchandise Bought) and 0.58% increase in Parts and Accessories GMB, primarily due to increase in conversion of high average price items in online experimentation.

</details>


### [14] [Scaling Graph Neural Networks for Friend Recommendation: Multi-Hash User Embeddings and Temporal Neighbor Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27413)
*Maksim Utushkin,Andrei Ovsiannikov,Alexander D'yakonov*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个用于生产级社交图谱（1.94亿用户、280亿条边）的可扩展端到端GNN排序系统，核心贡献是两项关键设计：多哈希ID嵌入（将嵌入表大小缩减98%以上）和时间邻居采样（通过时间戳排序CSR存储与二分查找，将采样复杂度从O(deg(v)+k)降至O(log(deg(v))+k)）。在线A/B测试显示好友推荐增加16%、独立好友添加者增加11.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 好友推荐本质上具有图结构特性，多跳社交上下文比用户属性更能决定潜在连接的关联性。但在生产规模（数亿用户、数百亿边）的社交图谱上部署消息传递GNN面临巨大的计算和系统挑战，尤其是高基数ID嵌入的存储问题（超过200GB）以及大度数节点的时间邻居采样效率问题。

Method: 提出两项核心设计：1) 多哈希ID嵌入（multi-hash ID embeddings）作为主要节点表示，将ID嵌入表大小减少98%以上同时保持排序质量；2) 时间戳排序CSR存储与二分查找（timestamp-sorted CSR storage with binary search）实现高效时间邻居采样，将每节点采样成本从O(deg(v)+k)降低到O(log(deg(v))+k)。系统为端到端可扩展的GNN排序框架，支持大规模分布式训练与推理。

Result: 在1.94亿用户、280亿边的图上，离线消融实验分别验证了每项设计的贡献。在线A/B测试中，相比强生产基线，好友推荐带来的好友增加提升16%，独立好友添加者提升11.5%。

Conclusion: 多哈希嵌入与时间邻居采样的组合在生产级社交图GNN系统中可扩展且具有可衡量的生产影响，该框架已开源用于大规模时间图的分布式训练和推理。

Abstract: Friend recommendation is inherently graph-structured: the relevance of a potential connection depends on multi-hop social context rather than user attributes alone. However, deploying message-passing GNNs on a production-scale social graph with hundreds of millions of users and tens of billions of edges requires addressing numerous modeling and systems challenges. We present a scalable end-to-end GNN ranking system for production social graphs, focusing on two design choices that are critical in this setting: multi-hash ID embeddings and temporal neighbor sampling. Multi-hash embeddings are common for high-cardinality features, but industrial GNN systems typically either ignore trainable IDs or accept full embedding tables, exceeding 200 GB for our graph. We integrate multi-hash as the primary node representation, reducing the ID-embedding table size by more than 98 percent while preserving ranking quality. Temporal neighbor sampling is well understood in principle, but existing implementations scan full adjacency lists, which is a non-starter for users with tens of thousands of friends. We implement timestamp-sorted CSR storage with binary search, reducing the per-node temporal sampling cost from $O(deg(v) + k)$ to $O(\log(deg(v)) + k)$. Beyond these components, we show that this combination scales and yields measurable production impact. On a graph with 194M users and 28B edges, offline ablations isolate each design choice's contribution. In an online A/B test, our system increases friend additions from recommendations by 16 percent and unique friend adders by 11.5 percent over a strong production baseline. We release our framework for distributed training and inference on large temporal graphs.

</details>


### [15] [misi: a Metric Inverted Sample Index](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27422)
*Edgar Chavez*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出misi，一种针对一般度量空间的近似最近邻搜索倒排索引，其词表为数据库的随机样本（规模与n成正比）。每个对象由其kb个最近样本点表示，查询通过idf加权共享邻居投票和精确验证C个候选来完成。该方法将NAPP索引从常数个枢轴推广到线性规模的词表，使倒排列表保持常数期望长度，并赋予索引"组合器"特性：任何在αn点上的高召回索引都可转化为n点上的索引。概率模型给出召回保证与匹配下界。设计强项在结构层面：构建过程为n个独立搜索，天然可并行、确定性，在64核上处理10^8向量仅需5250秒，比匹配召回率的图构建快3.7倍，可在3 GiB内存上限内流式执行。代价在查询时间：饱和图基线的RAM查询快6-16倍，0.99召回率的验证预算随n^0.30增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有近似最近邻搜索索引在一般度量空间中面临可扩展性和资源限制的挑战。NAPP索引使用常数个枢轴，其表达能力受限。作者希望构造一种规模与数据量线性相关的词表倒排索引，在保持高召回率的同时实现确定性、可并行、低内存占用的构建与查询，适用于频繁重建语料库、批量相似性负载和受限内存服务等场景。

Method: misi索引的核心方法包括：(1) 词表为数据库的n比例随机样本（规模αn，与n成正比）；(2) 每个对象通过可插拔的内部索引找到其kb个最近样本点来表征；(3) 构建过程为n次独立搜索，天然可并行且确定性；(4) 查询通过idf加权共享邻居投票产生C个候选，再精确验证；(5) 通过概率模型保证kb取n的对数量级即可实现所需召回率，且验证预算由索引自行估计；(6) 提供匹配下限证明：投票无法分辨低于1/√kb阶的overlap差异。

Result: 实验结果表明：构建10^8向量仅需5250秒（64核），比匹配召回率的图构建快3.7倍；可在3 GiB内存上限内流式运行；从NVMe为10^8向量提供查询服务时仅需8 GB强制预算，低于SSD图基线的运行下限。代价是查询时间：饱和图基线在RAM中查询快6-16倍，0.99召回率的验证预算随n^0.30增长。所有结果带有种子、饱和扫描和完整配置，由运行清单生成，包含实测负面结果。

Conclusion: misi的设计优势在于结构层面——构建是n个独立搜索，具备天然并行性、确定性、低内存占用和流式能力，并可将任何度量空间上的高召回索引"组合"为更大规模数据上的索引。其代价是查询时间的增加。该索引特别适合构建成本、确定性、内存足迹或黑盒度量比峰值吞吐更重要的应用场景。

Abstract: We present misi, an inverted index for approximate nearest-neighbor search over general metric spaces whose vocabulary is a random sample of the database, of size proportional to $n$. Each object is represented by its $k_b$ nearest sample points, found by a pluggable inner index over the sample; queries are answered by an idf-weighted shared-neighbor vote followed by exact verification of $C$ candidates. The construction generalizes the NAPP index from a constant number of pivots to a linear-size vocabulary, which keeps posting lists at constant expected length $ρ= k_b/α$ as $n$ grows and turns the index into a combinator: any high-recall index on $αn$ points yields an index on $n$ points, for any metric. A probabilistic model gives a recall guarantee -- $k_b$ logarithmic in $n$ over the overlap gap suffices, with a verification budget the index itself estimates -- and a matching limit: the vote cannot resolve overlap differences below order $1/\sqrt{k_b}$. The design's strengths are structural: construction is $n$ independent searches -- embarrassingly parallel, deterministic, $5{,}250$ s for $10^8$ vectors on 64 cores, $3.7\times$ faster than a matched-recall graph build -- it streams under an enforced 3 GiB cap, and the portable artifact serves $10^8$ vectors from NVMe within an enforced 8 GB budget, below the working floor of the SSD-graph baseline. Its cost is query-time work: saturated graph baselines answer $6$-$16\times$ faster in RAM, and the verification budget for 0.99 recall grows as $n^{0.30}$. All results carry seeds, saturation sweeps and full configurations, are generated from run manifests, and include measured negative results. The intended applications weight construction cost, determinism, memory footprint, or black-box metrics over peak throughput: frequently rebuilt corpora, batch similarity workloads, constrained-memory serving.

</details>

### [16] [High Probability Derivative Bounds for Random tanh Neural Networks on a Hypercube](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26526)
*Josef Dick,Michael Feischl,Fabian Zehetgruber*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为宽随机神经网络（尤其是tanh网络配合Xavier初始化）的混合输入导数建立了高概率界，证明在宽度足够大时，一阶导数界与深度无关，高阶混合导数最多以多项式L^{|u|-1}增长，并衍生出Lipschitz常数与加权Sobolev范数的高概率界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宽随机神经网络的导数界对理解其正则性与训练动态至关重要，但基于欧几里得算子范数的确定性分析会导致导数界随深度呈指数增长，过于悲观。作者希望为高斯权重的宽网络建立更精确的导数估计，揭示其真实的正则性结构。

Method: 通过分离最高阶导数中的线性项，并利用可测有限网控制相应的切向方向，对满足阶乘增长条件的激活导数的宽随机网络进行高概率分析。结合tanh激活与Xavier初始化，推导出关于混合导数|D^u R_{Φ^{(L)}}(x)|的显式上界。

Result: 证明了当隐藏宽度n满足n ≥ C(L^3 n_0^2(1+log n_0)+L^2(1+log(L/η)))时，以至少1-η的概率，|D^u R_{Φ^{(L)}}(x)| ≤ C_0|u|!(C_1L)^{|u|-1}∏_{j∈u}β_j(η,n_0) 对所有非空u⊆[n_0]及x∈[0,1]^{n_0}同时成立。一阶导数界不依赖深度，高阶导数至多按L^{|u|-1}多项式增长。由此进一步得到欧几里得Lipschitz常数和加权Sobolev范数的高概率界。

Conclusion: 该结果将导数估计与拟蒙特卡洛(QMC)积分相关联，表明网络正则性可以进入QMC训练的分析框架，为理解基于QMC训练的理论性质提供了新的途径。

Abstract: We establish high-probability bounds for mixed input derivatives of wide random neural networks whose activation derivatives satisfy a factorial growth bound. Our main result specializes these estimates to $\tanh$ networks with Xavier initialization. A direct deterministic analysis based on Euclidean operator norms of the weight matrices yields derivative bounds that generally grow exponentially with the depth. We show that this growth can be substantially improved for sufficiently wide Gaussian networks by isolating the term that is linear in the highest-order derivative and controlling the corresponding tangent directions by measurable finite nets.
  For scalar-output $\tanh$ networks with Gaussian weights and Xavier initialization, we prove that there exist constants $C,C_0,C_1>0$ such that, whenever the common hidden width satisfies $n \geq C\left(L^3n_0^2(1+\log n_0)+L^2\left(1+\log(L/η)\right)\right)$, then, with probability at least $1-η$, the estimate $\left|D^u\mathcal{R}_{Φ^{(L)}}(x)\right| \leq C_0 |u|! (C_1L)^{|u|-1}\prod_{j\in u}β_j(η,n_0)$ holds simultaneously for every non-empty $u\subseteq[n_0]$ and every $x\in[0,1]^{n_0}$. Thus, the first-order derivative bound is independent of the depth, while a square-free mixed derivative of order $|u|$ grows at most polynomially as $L^{|u|-1}$, apart from the coordinate factors. As consequences, we obtain high-probability bounds for the Euclidean Lipschitz constant and for weighted Sobolev norms of the network realization. The latter connect the derivative estimates to quasi-Monte Carlo integration and indicate how such regularity can enter the analysis of QMC-based training.

</details>


### [17] [SLM-Conditioned Hierarchical Relation Routing for Labeled Property Graph Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26132)
*Michal Podstawski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SLM-Conditioned Hierarchical Relation Routing（SLM条件化分层关系路由），一种将小型语言模型直接集成到图消息选择中的架构。该架构通过拓扑GNN提供稳定的结构表示和预测锚点，利用参数高效的SLM处理结构化图软令牌并生成目标条件的路由查询，在关系类型内部和跨关系类型两个层级上选择相关消息，最终提供对拓扑锚点的有界残差更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 带标签的属性图将关系结构与异质的文本和类别属性相结合，但传统图神经网络将属性表示为静态特征向量，无法确定哪些语义证据应影响特定预测目标的消息传播。现有方法缺乏对语义信息在消息传递过程中的动态选择能力。

Method: 架构由两部分组成：1）拓扑GNN提供稳定结构表示作为预测锚点；2）参数高效的SLM处理结构化图软令牌，为每个目标节点生成条件路由查询。消息组合包含邻居结构状态、节点属性编码、关系属性编码和关系类型。路由在两个层级进行：先在同一关系类型内选择相关消息，再跨关系类型路由信息到关系级摘要上。最终输出是对拓扑锚点的有界残差更新。

Result: 该架构在邻居层面和关系类型层面都支持可解释性分析，提供了将语言派生语义集成到属性丰富图学习中的通用机制，同时保留了结构证据。

Conclusion: SLM条件化分层关系路由为属性图学习提供了新范式，通过将小型语言模型融入消息选择过程，实现了语义感知的条件化图消息路由，兼顾了结构稳定性与语义灵活性。

Abstract: Labeled property graphs combine relational structure with heterogeneous textual and categorical properties attached to both nodes and relationships. Conventional graph neural networks typically represent these properties as static feature vectors, limiting their ability to determine which semantic evidence should influence message propagation for a particular prediction target. We propose SLM-Conditioned Hierarchical Relation Routing, an architecture that integrates a small language model directly into graph message selection. A topology GNN provides a stable structural representation and prediction anchor. For each target node, incident messages combine the neighbor's structural state, node-property encoding, relationship-property encoding, and relationship type. A parameter-efficient SLM processes structured graph soft tokens and produces a target-conditioned routing query. This query first selects relevant messages within each relationship type and subsequently routes information across relation-level summaries. The resulting representation provides a bounded residual update to the topology anchor, preserving structural evidence while allowing contextual semantic information to modify the prediction. The architecture supports interpretable analysis at both the neighbor and relationship-type levels and provides a general mechanism for integrating language-derived semantics into property-rich graph learning.

</details>


### [18] [NeuronFuzz: Safety Neuron Guided Fuzzing for LLM Safety Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26222)
*Zhiyuan Xu,Muhammad Firhard Roslan,Joseph Gardiner,Sana Belguith,Lichao Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: NeuronFuzz是一个白盒模糊测试框架，利用LLM内部的安全神经元激活值作为连续执行反馈来进行安全评估，无需生成目标模型的响应即可高效地发现越狱攻击模板，在21个文本和多模态模型上实现了76-100%的越狱发现率，明显优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自动化LLM安全测试方法依赖响应级别的反馈，每个候选越狱提示都需要生成目标模型的响应来评估攻击效果，这不仅成本高昂，而且对强对齐模型提供的信息稀疏——因为大多数候选提示都会被拒绝，产生相同的失败结果，难以指导有效的模糊测试。

Method: NeuronFuzz首先通过SafetyOracle将安全神经元的激活值转换为连续的"安全警报分数"，该分数可在prefill阶段获得，从而将响应生成从模糊测试循环中移除。SafetyOracle的构建采用模板不变的恶意/良性输入和稳定性感知选择，识别出紧凑的安全神经元集合。由于警报分数可微分，NeuronFuzz利用其梯度识别安全敏感的模板位置，并结合掩码语言模型生成流畅且上下文兼容的突变，同时保留原始恶意载荷。

Result: 在5个白盒源模型上实现76-100%的越狱发现率，比基线方法高出最多48个百分点。优化后的模板可以零样本迁移到开源权重和6个专有目标模型，平均ASR和top-5集成ASR(EASR)分别达到69.6%/92.6%和44.1%/60.0%。

Conclusion: NeuronFuzz证明利用内部安全神经元作为连续反馈信号可以显著提升LLM安全评估的效率和有效性，同时生成的越狱模板具有良好的跨模型迁移能力，为白盒安全测试提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Safety evaluation is critical for assessing whether aligned Large Language Models (LLMs) remain robust against jailbreak attacks. Existing automated testing methods, however, largely rely on response-level feedback: each candidate prompt typically requires generating a target-model response to evaluate its attack effectiveness. This process is expensive and, more importantly, provides only sparse guidance on strongly aligned models, where most candidates are rejected with the same failure outcome.
  This paper presents NeuronFuzz, a white-box fuzzing framework that exploits internal safety neurons as continuous execution feedback for LLM safety evaluation. A SafetyOracle converts safety-neuron activations into a continuous safety alarm score that serves as feedback for fuzzing and can be obtained during prefill, eliminating response generation from the fuzzing loop. To construct the SafetyOracle, NeuronFuzz uses template-invariant harmful and benign inputs and stability-aware selection to identify a compact set of safety neurons whose activations capture harmful-intent recognition. Moreover, since the safety alarm score is differentiable, NeuronFuzz uses its gradients to identify safety-sensitive template positions and a masked language model to generate fluent, context-compatible mutations while preserving original harmful payload and avoiding additional optimization variables. We evaluate NeuronFuzz across 21 text and multimodal models. Across five white-box source models, it achieves a 76-100% jailbreak discovery rate, outperforming baselines by up to 48 percentage points. Its optimized templates further transfer zero-shot to open-weight and six proprietary target models, achieving average ASR and top-5 ensemble ASR (EASR) of 69.6%/92.6% and 44.1%/60.0%, respectively.

</details>


### [19] [Pruning Binarized Neural Networks: A Dedicated Framework and Globally Weighted Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26233)
*Roan Rubiales,Jean Pierre David*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于PyTorch的研究型框架，结合冻结和剪枝机制用于设计和优化二值化神经网络（BNN），并引入了一种跨抽象层级考虑参数相对重要性的全局权重剪枝方法，在VGG11上实现了70%的剪枝率且精度保持不变，远超现有最先进的41%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络的极端压缩（直至完全二值化）可显著降低内存占用和计算复杂度，便于在FPGA和微控制器等受限边缘硬件上部署。虽然将二值化与剪枝结合有望带来额外的效率提升，但现有剪枝策略不适配二值化表示，且很少能转化为实际的硬件收益。

Method: 提出一个基于PyTorch的研究型框架，集成了冻结（freezing）和剪枝（pruning）机制用于BNN设计和优化。在该框架基础上，提出一种新颖的剪枝方法，考虑学习参数在不同抽象层级（abstraction levels）上的相对重要性，通过全局加权机制实现精度与剪枝率之间的更优权衡。

Result: 所提出的全局加权机制在精度与剪枝率之间取得了优越的权衡：在VGG11上以恒定精度实现70%的剪枝率，而二值化设置下的最先进结果仅为41%。

Conclusion: 该框架能够快速可复现地评估现有最先进方法并快速原型化新方法；所提出的基于跨层相对重要性的全局剪枝策略显著优于现有二值化场景下的剪枝方法。

Abstract: Extreme compression of deep neural networks, up to full binarization, dramatically reduces memory footprint and arithmetic complexity, facilitating deployment on constrained edge hardware with field-programmable gate arrays (FPGAs) and microcontrollers. Although combining binarization with pruning promises additional efficiency gains, existing pruning strategies are ill-suited to binarized representations and rarely translate into meaningful hardware savings. We introduce a PyTorch-based, research-oriented framework that incorporates freezing and pruning mechanisms for designing and optimizing binarized neural networks. The framework enables rapid and reproducible evaluation of state-of-the-art approaches and the fast prototyping of new ones. Leveraging this framework, we propose a novel pruning method that accounts for the relative importance of learned parameters across abstraction levels. Such a global weighting mechanism consistently achieves a superior trade-off between model accuracy and pruning rate, achieving a 70% pruning rate on VGG11 with constant accuracy, while state-of-the-art results reach only 41% in the binarized setting.

</details>


### [20] [Muon with Finite Newton-Schulz: The Smoothing Benefit in Nonsmooth Nonconvex Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26288)
*Mingyi Li,Taira Tsuchiya*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明Muon优化器中有限深度的Newton-Schulz迭代不仅是近似误差，反而能为非光滑非凸优化带来益处。通过在线到非凸转换的分析框架，作者证明仅需对数级别的Newton-Schulz深度即可在非光滑非凸优化中收敛到驻点，而使用精确极分解的Muon反而可能无法收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有理论要么用精确极因子替代Muon实际使用的有限Newton-Schulz迭代，要么将其视为近似误差，导致理论上有限深度迭代只能损害收敛保证。本文旨在纠正这一认知，表明有限Newton-Schulz迭代在非光滑非凸优化中实际上是有益的。

Method: 采用在线到非凸转换（online-to-nonconvex conversion）框架分析Muon：将更新规则视为在线学习器，将其遗憾界转换为驻点保证。关键洞见是有限Newton-Schulz迭代将不连续的极映射平滑为关于奇异值的Lipschitz映射，使Muon可视为带平滑谱势的在线学习器；并证明Newton-Schulz深度仅需随目标精度对数增长即可保证收敛。

Result: 证明了Newton-Schulz深度只需在目标精度上取对数级别，即可保证非光滑非凸优化收敛到驻点；样本复杂度匹配非光滑非凸优化的已知最优界，且在光滑非凸优化中达到问题依赖因子意义下的最优。此外，论证可推广到具有相同平滑性质的一般谱映射。

Conclusion: 有限Newton-Schulz迭代的平滑效应是Muon理论分析的关键，它解决了精确极分解不连续性导致的收敛失败问题，并为Muon在非光滑非凸场景下的收敛性提供了首个正面保证。

Abstract: Muon has emerged as a strong optimizer for the matrix-valued parameters in large language model pretraining, approximately orthogonalizing its momentum with a few Newton-Schulz iterations. Existing theory either replaces this iteration with the exact polar factor it approximates, or treats its finite depth as an approximation error, and thus the iteration Muon actually runs can only hurt the guarantees. We show that finite Newton-Schulz can instead be beneficial for nonsmooth nonconvex optimization. To this end, we analyze Muon through the online-to-nonconvex conversion, which views the update rule as an online learner and converts its regret bound into a stationarity guarantee. The finite Newton-Schulz iteration smooths the discontinuous polar map into a Lipschitz map of the singular values, and Muon with finite Newton-Schulz can be regarded as an online learner with a smoothed spectral potential. This smoothing is exactly what the conversion needs: we prove that a Newton-Schulz depth growing only logarithmically in the target accuracy suffices for convergence to stationary points in nonsmooth nonconvex optimization, whereas Muon with the exact-polar update may fail to converge. The resulting sample complexity bounds match the best-known guarantees for nonsmooth nonconvex optimization and are optimal for smooth nonconvex optimization up to problem-dependent factors. The argument extends beyond Newton-Schulz to general spectral maps with the same smoothing property.

</details>


### [21] [Algebraic Multigrid Acceleration for Efficient Label Spreading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26309)
*Antonia van Betteray,Jonathan Klees,Miriam Schäfers,Matthias Rottmann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出AMELS框架，通过快速邻域图构建和代数多重网格求解器加速标签传播（Label Spreading）半监督学习，使其能够高效扩展到大规模高维数据集，在显著降低运行时间的同时保持或提升分类精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代机器学习依赖大量标注数据，但人工标注昂贵耗时。标签传播作为半监督学习方法可缓解此问题，但其在大规模高维数据集上的应用受限于计算成本和内存约束。

Method: 提出AMELS框架，包含两个关键创新：1) 快速构建邻域图以提高图构建效率；2) 引入代数多重网格（Algebraic Multigrid）求解器替代传统标签传播中的随机游走迭代，利用多重网格的多级特性在单个多重网格周期内完成跨任意大小图的信息传播。

Result: AMELS相比现有实现实现了显著的运行时间减少，同时对超参数选择更鲁棒（在运行时间和分类精度两方面均如此），在仅有少量标注样本的情况下也能在大规模图像数据集上产生准确的标签。

Conclusion: AMELS框架使标签传播能够高效应用于大规模图像数据集，即使在只有少数标注样本的条件下也能生成准确标签，为半监督学习在大规模场景下的实际应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Modern machine learning models rely on large amounts of labeled data. However, manual annotation of large-scale datasets is expensive and time-consuming. Label spreading is a semi-supervised learning technique that addresses this challenge by propagating information from a few labeled examples to a larger pool of unlabeled data. Despite its effectiveness, its application to large-scale, high-dimensional datasets is limited by computational costs and memory constraints. To address these limitations, we propose Algebraic Multigrid Acceleration for Efficient Label Spreading (AMELS), an efficient label spreading framework that improves scalability by fast construction of neighborhood graphs and the incorporation of algebraic multigrid solvers. The latter is an iterative solver that replaces the ordinary random walk iteration typically performed in label spreading. Due to the multilevel nature of algebraic multigrid solvers, AMELS spreads given label information across a graph of any size in a single multigrid cycle. We demonstrate that AMELS achieves significant runtime reductions compared to existing implementations while also being more robust to hyperparameter choices in terms of both runtime and classification accuracy. Our framework therefore enables efficient label spreading on large-scale image datasets and produces accurate labels even when only a few labeled samples are available.

</details>


### [22] [Privacy Without Regret: Differentially Private Inference-Time Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26324)
*Ishi Jain,Nandini Bhattad,Sayak Ray Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出通过向奖励分数添加校准噪声这一单一干预手段，同时解决推理时对齐（Best-of-N采样）中的奖励黑客（reward hacking）和敏感人类偏好数据的隐私保护两大问题，分别提出PrivBoN和PrivITP两种方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Best-of-N (BoN)采样是最广泛使用的推理时对齐策略，但存在两个缺陷：一是奖励黑客问题，即选中的响应可能利用代理奖励模型的错误；二是训练奖励模型所依赖的敏感人类偏好数据缺乏隐私保护。作者寻求一种单一干预同时解决这两个问题。

Method: 核心思路是向奖励分数添加校准噪声后再进行选择。具体提出两种方法：1) PrivBoN：以适当尺度的Gumbel噪声实现ε-差分隐私，同时实现KL正则化对齐；2) PrivITP：结合χ²正则化拒绝采样和两阶段高斯机制，实现ex-post (ε,δ)-DP，其隐私代价与响应数量n无关，并将正则化参数与隐私参数解耦。

Result: 理论结果表明：当隐私预算超过临界阈值ε*时，隐私要求的噪声即为遗憾最优的正则化，隐私不产生额外的对齐代价，匹配Huang等人(2025)的信息论下界。实验验证了PrivBoN和PrivITP具有缩放单调性（不同于BoN在临界n后退化），且PrivITP在相同隐私水平下匹配或优于PrivBoN，在强隐私场景下增益最大。

Conclusion: 通过向奖励分数添加校准噪声，可以同时实现差分隐私保护和KL正则化对齐，从而统一解决BoN采样中的奖励黑客与数据隐私两大问题，并且在高隐私预算下不会产生额外的对齐成本。

Abstract: Best-of-N (BoN) sampling is the simplest and most widely deployed inference-time alignment strategy, but it suffers from two distinct problems: reward hacking, in which the selected response exploits errors in the proxy reward model, and the absence of any privacy protection for the sensitive human preference data used to train that reward model. We show that a single intervention-adding calibrated noise to reward scores before selection-resolves both. Our first result, Private Best-of-N (PrivBoN), establishes that Gumbel noise at an appropriate scale simultaneously provides $ε$-differential privacy and implements KL-regularized alignment. Whenever the privacy budget exceeds a critical threshold $ε^*$, the privacy-mandated noise is the regret-optimal regularization, and privacy imposes zero additional alignment cost-matching the information-theoretic skyline of Huang et al. (2025). Because $ε^*$ depends on an unknown coverage coefficient, we introduce Private Inference-Time Pessimism (PrivITP), which combines $χ^2$-regularized rejection sampling with a two-phase Gaussian mechanism. PrivITP achieves ex-post $(ε,δ)$-DP with a privacy cost independent of the number of responses $n$, cleanly decouples the regularization parameter from the privacy parameter, and attains the skyline up to a noise-inflation term. Experiments across several language models, datasets, and reward models confirm our results: PrivBoN and PrivITP are scaling-monotonic (unlike BoN, which degrades past a critical $n$), and PrivITP matches or outperforms PrivBoN at equivalent privacy levels, with the largest gains in the strong-privacy regime.

</details>


### [23] [Beyond Capability Benchmarks: Learning Operational Fingerprints of LLM Cloud Services from Production Incident Metadata](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26332)
*Meiwei Zhang,Eduardo Miranda,Bruce Baynes,Suvigya Jain,Wanlong Chen,Tao He,Sergey Borodavkin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出OpEmbed框架，利用结构化且隐私保护的工单元数据（不含案例文本），为LLM云服务学习紧凑的"操作指纹"。在Google Cloud超过33,000个生产支持工单、7个LLM家族、26个月的数据上验证，能够恢复可解释的家族/版本结构、改善留一模型的操作预测、在有限早期窗口数据下依然有效，并支持跨模型故障类型迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 托管LLM服务已投入真实生产系统，但模型选择和服务规划仍依赖能力基准测试，这些基准几乎无法揭示部署后的操作行为（如故障、稳定性、支持需求等），导致生产环境中的运维决策缺乏可靠依据。

Method: OpEmbed将模型-时间窗口聚合为八通道操作签名，并通过时间对比学习、跨视图重建和世代序性正则化学习低维表示。整个过程仅使用结构化、隐私保护的支持工单元数据，不依赖案例文本内容。

Result: 在Google Cloud超过33,000个生产支持工单（7个LLM家族、26个月）的评估中，OpEmbed恢复了可解释的家族与版本级结构，在留一模型操作预测中优于非学习基线，在有限早期窗口数据下仍保持有用性，并支持跨模型的故障类型迁移。

Conclusion: OpEmbed为LLM云服务的模型上线、支持就绪评估和操作监控提供了实用的嵌入学习工具，作者总结了从构建和评估该工具过程中获得的实践经验教训。

Abstract: Managed LLM services are now part of real production systems, but model selection and service planning still rely heavily on capability benchmarks that reveal little about operational behavior after deployment. We present Operational Embedding (OpEmbed), a framework for learning compact operational fingerprints of LLM cloud services from structured, privacy-preserving support-case metadata, without using case text. OpEmbed aggregates model--time windows into an eight-channel operational signature and learns a low-dimensional representation via temporal contrastive learning, cross-view reconstruction, and generational-ordinality regularization. Evaluated on more than 33,000 production support cases spanning seven LLM families over 26 months at Google Cloud, OpEmbed recovers interpretable family- and version-level structure, improves leave-one-model-out operational forecasting over non-learned baselines, remains useful under limited early-window data, and supports cross-model fault-type transfer. We report the practical lessons learned from building and evaluating this tool for model onboarding, support readiness assessment, and operational monitoring.

</details>


### [24] [CG4AI: A Column Generation Framework for Training AI Models Under Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26375)
*Youcef Magnouche,Abderrahmane Driouch,Sébastien Martin,Pierre Bauguion*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CG4AI框架，通过凸组合多个AI模型并用线性规划求解最优权重，同时结合列生成和割平面法，确保模型输出满足线性约束。在MNIST分类和多商品流路由问题上验证，能够生成可行预测且精度优于单模型基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准机器学习只最小化损失函数，无法保证模型输出满足预定义规则或约束。在自动驾驶、网络路由等应用中，这种可行性保证至关重要，因此需要一种在训练过程中显式纳入约束的框架。

Method: CG4AI框架包含三个核心组件：(1) 主线性规划(LP)求解模型组合的凸权重；(2) 定价子问题利用LP对偶变量生成新模型，聚焦于最被违反的约束；(3) 割平面过程将可行性保证扩展到训练集之外。整体通过列生成思想迭代构建模型组合。

Result: 在MNIST上展示了四种约束用途：仅从约束学习、提升对抗鲁棒性、修正误分类样本、强制输出重标记；在多商品流问题中对神经网络路由预测器强制链路容量约束。在MNIST和SNDLIB基准网络上验证，CG4AI可靠地生成可行预测，精度优于单模型基线。

Conclusion: CG4AI通过凸组合与列生成、割平面法的结合，为带约束的AI模型训练提供了有效方案，在可行性和精度上均优于传统单模型方法，具有广泛的实际应用价值。

Abstract: Standard machine-learning training minimizes a loss function over a dataset, but does not guarantee that the resulting model will satisfy predefined rules or constraints on its outputs. In many real-world applications, ranging from autonomous systems to network routing, such guarantees are essential. We propose CG4AI, a framework that builds a convex combination of AI models while enforcing linear constraints on the combined output. A master linear program (LP) determines the optimal mixture weights, while a pricing subproblem generates new models guided by LP dual variables, focusing attention on the most violated constraints. A cutting-plane procedure extends feasibility guarantees beyond the training set. We apply CG4AI to two problems: (i) digit classification on MNIST, where we demonstrate four distinct uses of constraints, learning from constraints alone, improving adversarial robustness, correcting misclassified examples, and enforcing output relabeling; and (ii) the multi-commodity flow problem, where link capacity constraints are enforced on neural-network routing predictors. Experiments on MNIST and standard SNDLIB benchmark networks show that CG4AI reliably produces feasible predictors while achieving better accuracy than single-model baselines.

</details>


### [25] [The Latent Diagnostic Taxonomy: A Framework for Constructing Classifiers and Diagnosing Their Decisions, Applied to Prompt Injection Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26423)
*Jaturong Kongmanee,Smile Thanapattheerakul*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Latent Diagnostic Taxonomy框架，通过降维优化的分类器、潜在支持向量定位和诊断分类学，识别分类器自信决策中不可信的判断，并将脆弱性分为启发式偏差、启发式覆盖和不充分上下文三类，以指导不同的处理策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在安全关键场景（如提示注入检测）中，分类器的自信判断可能因单个token的移除而改变，这种脆弱性导致其决策不可信。现有工作缺乏一个系统性的端到端框架来诊断和区分不同类型的分类失败模式。

Method: 框架包含三步：(i)通过交叉验证优化嵌入维度而非先验固定，构建维度优化的分类器；(ii)定位约29%训练样本的潜在支持向量，作为影响预测标签的关键提示；(iii)利用这些token及其攻击幅度构建诊断分类学，将提示分为可安全依赖、启发式偏差/覆盖、上下文不足需人工审查三个区域。

Result: 在公开的提示注入数据集上，约77%的自信决策在移除单个token后不稳健。这种脆弱性分为两种模式：置信度校准失败和可被真正利用的捷径。框架为每个诊断区域提供了相应的补救策略建议。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一种端到端的诊断机制，不仅能识别分类器决策的不可靠性，还能区分其失败模式的本质（校准问题还是可被利用的捷径），并为不同类型的失败推荐差异化的处理策略。

Abstract: This paper proposes a framework for constructing a classifier as a safeguard layer, and for developing a complementary diagnostic that identifies which of the classifier's confident decisions can be trusted. This framework, the Latent Diagnostic Taxonomy, consists of (i) constructing a dimensionality-optimized classifier, in which the embedding dimensionality is empirically selected via cross-validated performance rather than fixed a priori, (ii) locating a relatively small set of latent support vectors (~ 29% of total training examples) representing influential prompts for identifying tokens that alter the classifier's predicted labels, and (iii) utilizing such tokens and their associated attack magnitudes for constructing a diagnostic taxonomy. This diagnostic taxonomy provides an end-to-end guideline for flagging prompts that require different treatments: rely Safely on the classifier's decision; flag Heuristic Bias and Heuristic Override cases; route Insufficient Context cases for further human/safety review. Applying the framework to a classifier trained on a public prompt injection dataset, we find that a substantial fraction of its confident decisions (~ 77%) are not robust to removing a single token, and that this brittleness separates into two distinct failure patterns: a confidence calibration failure and a genuinely exploitable shortcut. For each zone of the taxonomy, we also recommend strategies for remediating diagnosed prompts. We illustrate the framework as a series of steps, demonstrating how each step operates.

</details>


### [26] [FedCMAPSS: A Benchmark for Federated Learning in Remaining Useful Life Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26433)
*Amelia Sorrenti,Matteo Pennisi,Concetto Spampinato,Simone Palazzo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了FedCMAPSS，一个基于NASA C-MAPSS数据集的联邦剩余使用寿命（RUL）估计基准。该基准定义了五个标准化任务来模拟真实工业场景（从理想IID到极端统计异质性），并系统评估了多种联邦优化算法与多种神经网络架构的组合，同时公开源代码和数据划分以建立可复现的基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据驱动的预测性维护是工业4.0的关键支撑，但run-to-failure数据的稀缺限制了RUL估计模型的稳健发展。联邦学习虽然提供了无需共享传感器数据即可协作训练预测模型的范式，但目前研究缺乏统一的评估框架，导致不同方法之间难以公平比较。

Method: 基于NASA C-MAPSS数据集构建FedCMAPSS基准，定义五个标准化任务模拟从理想IID到极端统计异质性的真实工业挑战，并对多种最先进的联邦优化算法在多个神经网络架构上进行系统评估。

Result: 建立了可复现的基线，并公开了源代码和数据划分，为联邦预测性维护解决方案的开发与比较提供了标准基础。

Conclusion: 该基准填补了联邦RUL估计领域缺乏统一评估框架的空白，为后续研究提供了标准化平台，有助于推动联邦学习在工业预测性维护中的实际落地。

Abstract: Data-driven prognostics and health management has emerged as a key enabler for Industry 4.0, yet the development of robust remaining useful life (RUL) estimation models is often limited by the scarcity of run-to-failure data. While federated learning offers a promising paradigm to collaboratively train predictive models without sharing sensor data, research efforts have operated so far in the absence of a common evaluation framework. To address this gap, this paper introduces FedCMAPSS, a benchmark for federated RUL estimation based on the commonly-used NASA C-MAPSS dataset. We define a set of five standardized tasks designed to simulate real-world industrial challenges, ranging from ideal IID settings to extreme statistical heterogeneity, and conduct a systematic evaluation of state-of-the-art federated optimization algorithms across multiple neural architectures. By establishing reproducible baselines and making the source code and data splits publicly available, this work aims to provide a standard foundation for developing and comparing federated predictive maintenance solutions.

</details>


### [27] [NeoTriFuse: Reliability-Aware Multimodal Fusion under Missingness Heterogeneity for Neonatal Mortality Risk Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26436)
*Jiyuan Tian,Qincheng Shen,Ye Lin,Yu Gao,Haohui Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出NeoTriFuse，一种可靠性感知的多模态融合框架，用于处理新生儿死亡风险预测中的缺失值异构性问题。该框架将缺失性建模为显式可靠性信号，动态调节各模态在融合中的贡献，通过可靠性引导的门控机制整合静态围产期变量、局部-全局时间编码器和患者级统计摘要，并联合优化死亡预测与住院时长辅助目标。实验获得F1为0.6736±0.0216、AUROC为0.9454±0.0056的成绩，消融实验表明局部-全局时间架构和患者级统计摘要分支贡献最大，可靠性感知门控在异构观测完整性下对阈值依赖指标有额外提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新生儿死亡风险预测面临极端类别不平衡、临床风险因素异质、多尺度时间动态复杂以及大量数据缺失等挑战。现有多模态方法通常将缺失视为预处理问题而非建模对象，未能充分利用缺失信息。

Method: NeoTriFuse框架包含三个核心模块：(1)集成静态围产期变量、局部-全局时间编码器及患者级统计摘要；(2)通过可靠性引导的门控机制将缺失性建模为显式可靠性信号，动态调制各模态在融合中的贡献；(3)联合优化死亡预测主任务与住院时长辅助目标。

Result: NeoTriFuse取得F1分数0.6736±0.0216和AUROC 0.9454±0.0056。消融实验显示局部-全局时间架构和患者级统计摘要分支贡献最大，可靠性感知门控对阈值依赖指标有额外改进，敏感性分析表明在邻近超参数设置下性能稳定。

Conclusion: 可靠性感知的多模态融合方法在真实临床缺失条件下是新生儿死亡预测的实用方案，未来为临床实践提供了有价值的建模范式。

Abstract: Neonatal mortality risk prediction from bedside monitoring data remains challenging due to extreme class imbalance, heterogeneous clinical risk factors, multi-scale temporal dynamics, and substantial missingness. We propose NeoTriFuse, a reliability-aware multimodal fusion framework for missingness-heterogeneous neonatal monitoring data. Unlike conventional multimodal approaches that treat missingness primarily as a preprocessing issue, NeoTriFuse models missingness as an explicit reliability signal that dynamically modulates modality contributions during fusion. The framework integrates static perinatal variables, local-global temporal encoders, and patient-level statistical summaries through reliability-guided gating mechanisms, while jointly optimizing mortality prediction and an auxiliary length-of-stay objective. NeoTriFuse achieves competitive performance, with an F1 score of 0.6736 +/- 0.0216 and an AUROC of 0.9454 +/- 0.0056. Ablation studies indicate that the local-global temporal architecture and patient-level summary branch contribute most substantially to predictive performance, while reliability-aware gating provides additional improvements on threshold-dependent metrics under heterogeneous observation completeness. Sensitivity analyses further suggest stable performance across nearby hyperparameter settings. Overall, the findings support reliability-aware multimodal fusion as a practical approach for neonatal mortality prediction under realistic clinical missingness conditions.

</details>


### [28] [Subgraph Filtering for Fair Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26437)
*Haohui Lu,jiyuan Tian,Fangyu Zhou,Shahadat Uddin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SF-GNN框架，通过识别并随机过滤偏置传播边（结合敏感同质性和结构传播放大器），从源头缓解GNN的结构性不公平，在五个基准数据集上实现更好的公平-准确率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有公平感知GNN方法主要在全局层面约束表示或预测分布，未能显式控制偏置信息在聚合过程中通过局部结构路径传播的问题。偏置信息会通过图拓扑和消息传递在敏感同质性下传播，即使敏感属性未包含在节点特征中也会导致不公平行为。

Method: SF-GNN包含三个核心组件：(1) 偏置边识别：结合敏感同质性、枢纽参与度和三角闭包等结构传播放大器来识别易偏置边；(2) 随机边过滤：在每个消息传递步骤中对识别出的偏置边进行随机下加权或移除，同时保留其余图结构；(3) 训练优化：引入带预热调度的统计平价正则化器以稳定优化过程。该框架轻量且与架构无关。

Result: 在五个基准数据集上的实验表明，SF-GNN在保持竞争性预测性能的同时实现了一致的公平性提升，相比近年来的公平感知GNN基线方法获得了更好的公平-准确率权衡。

Conclusion: SF-GNN通过在信息传播源头（偏置边的结构性路径）进行干预而非仅做全局约束，为GNN公平性提供了更直接有效的解决方案，且具备架构无关性和轻量性优势。

Abstract: Graph neural networks (GNNs) can exhibit unfair behavior even when sensitive attributes are excluded from node features, because graph topology and message passing propagate group-correlated signals under sensitive homophily. Existing fairness-aware GNN methods mainly constrain representations or prediction distributions at a global level, without explicitly controlling the local structural pathways through which biased information propagates during aggregation. We propose Subgraph Filtering for Fair Graph Neural Networks (SF-GNN), a lightweight and architecture-agnostic framework that mitigates structural bias at its source. SF-GNN identifies bias-prone edges by combining sensitive homophily with structural propagation amplifiers, including hub participation and triadic closure. It then incorporates stochastic edge filtering into each message-passing step to selectively downweight or remove these edges while preserving the remaining graph structure. Training further incorporates a statistical-parity regularizer with a warm-up schedule to stabilize optimization. Experiments on five benchmark datasets show that SF-GNN achieves consistent fairness improvements while maintaining competitive predictive performance, leading to a better fairness--accuracy trade-off than recent fairness-aware GNN baselines.

</details>


### [29] [Toward Equitable Low-Carbon Mobility: Fairness-Aware Demand Prediction for Expanding Bike-Sharing Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26451)
*Man Luo,Yixuan Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FairGIN，一个面向扩张中共享单车系统的公平感知图神经网络，同时解决冷启动预测和公平资源分配问题，在NYC和Seattle数据集上实现了最先进的预测精度并显著降低收入不平等。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共享单车系统的持续扩张带来两个挑战：(1) 新部署站点缺乏历史骑行记录，导致图模型在动态网络上面临训练与推理不匹配的冷启动问题；(2) 历史需求数据可能编码结构性不平等，低收入社区的低骑行量反映的是基础设施接入受限而非潜在需求不足，直接训练会加剧已有的出行不公平。

Method: FairGIN包含三个核心组件：(1) Expansion-Simulated Increment Training——在训练过程中随机模拟网络扩张，缩小冷启动分布差距；(2) Attention-Based Knowledge Transfer——结合站点自适应温度缩放与正交嵌入对齐，将数据丰富的现有站点表征迁移到新站点；(3) Fairness-Aware Optimization——引入按收入分层（income-stratified）的正则化项和公平校准的部署评分，促进更具包容性的站点布局。

Result: 在NYC和Seattle数据集上的实验表明，FairGIN在多样化的扩张场景下实现了最先进的预测精度，同时在不大幅牺牲系统整体效率的前提下，显著降低了基于收入的出行差异。

Conclusion: 公平感知的GNN可以同时兼顾预测准确性与资源分配公平性，为扩张中的共享单车系统提供了兼顾效率和公平的解决方案。

Abstract: Bike-sharing systems are an important component of low-carbon urban mobility, but continued expansion creates challenges in both cold-start prediction and equitable resource allocation. Newly deployed stations lack historical ridership records, causing a mismatch between training and inference for graph-based models on evolving networks. Historical demand may also encode structural inequalities, as lower ridership in low-income neighborhoods can reflect limited infrastructure access rather than weak latent demand. Models trained directly on such data may therefore reinforce existing mobility disparities. We propose FairGIN, a fairness-aware graph neural network for demand prediction in expanding bike-sharing systems. FairGIN integrates three components. Expansion-Simulated Increment Training stochastically simulates network expansion during training to reduce the cold-start distribution gap. Attention-Based Knowledge Transfer combines station-adaptive temperature scaling with orthogonal embedding alignment to transfer representations from data-rich existing stations to data-sparse new stations. Fairness-Aware Optimization introduces income-stratified regularization and an equity-calibrated deployment score to support more inclusive station placement. Experiments on NYC and Seattle demonstrate that FairGIN achieves state-of-the-art predictive accuracy across diverse expansion scenarios while substantially reducing income-based disparities without compromising overall system efficiency.

</details>
