Method: 遵循PRISMA指南，系统检索五个数据库（2022年1月至2026年3月），围绕四个综述问题筛选文献。从4,512条记录中纳入122项原始研究和12份监管/标准文件（作为原始来源分析）。采用社会技术视角，将问责构建为"行为者-论坛"关系并拆解为五个维度；通过四层分类装置（溯源、应用逻辑、人类监督、治理与救济）分析语料，并以可追溯性、角色清晰度、持续监控三个横切维度交叉审视；将框架映射至欧盟AI法案、NIST AI RMF及生成式AI配置文件、ISO/IEC 42001和多个行业指南，进行成熟度评估。

Result: 综述综合出问责的四类机制族（技术控制、人类监督、组织治理、文档与可追溯性），每类附成熟度评估。识别出四个持续存在的空白：人类监督规范不足、缺乏共享问责指标、学科割裂、经验评估有限；以及五个没有任何被调查工具能解决的结构性张力。最终将分类装置整合为LAAF问责架构，网络安全对齐OWASP LLM Top 10（2025）。需注意该架构是证据综合而非已验证的产物。

Conclusion: LLM问责制是一个跨技术、人、组织和制度的复杂社会技术问题。当前监管框架（如欧盟AI法案2026年8月生效的高风险义务）和标准（NIST AI RMF、ISO/IEC 42001）提供了基础但存在系统性的空白和未解决的张力。作者提出的LAAF架构作为综合框架，为后续研究和实践提供了统一的问责分类和机制蓝图，但需要进一步的实证验证和跨学科协作来填补识别出的空白。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) operate in hospitals, courtrooms, banks, and public service desks, where fluent, confident outputs are treated as authoritative even when ungrounded or incorrect. When such an output contributes to harm, who is answerable, and through what mechanisms can responsibility be traced, explained, and acted upon? Following PRISMA guidance, five databases were searched from January 2022 to March 2026 against four review questions; of 4,512 records identified, 122 primary studies were included, together with 12 regulatory and standards documents analysed as primary sources. The review consolidates a sociotechnical account of accountability as an actor-forum relation resolved into five dimensions, and synthesises mechanisms across four families: technical controls, human oversight, organisational governance, and documentation and traceability, each with a maturity assessment. The corpus is read through a four-layer classification device spanning provenance, application logic, human oversight, and governance and redress, cross-cut by traceability, role clarity, and continuous monitoring. Both are mapped onto the EU AI Act, whose high-risk obligations have applied since 2 August 2026, the NIST AI RMF with its Generative AI Profile, ISO/IEC 42001, and sectoral guidance in healthcare, consumer finance, education, and the public sector. Four persistent gaps emerge: under-specification of human oversight, absence of shared accountability metrics, disciplinary disconnection, and limited empirical evaluation, alongside five structural tensions that no surveyed instrument resolves. The review closes by consolidating the classification device into an integrated accountability architecture, LAAF, with cybersecurity aligned to the OWASP LLM Top 10 (2025); it is a synthesis of the surveyed evidence rather than a validated artefact.

</details>


### [173] [TransMeme: A Multi-Agent Framework for Cross-Cultural Meme Transcreation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27127)
*Jingyi Zheng,Yule Liu,Zifan Peng,Tianyi Hu,Yuemeng Zhao,Xinhu Zheng,Xinlei He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多智能体框架用于跨文化网络迷因（meme）的创译（transcreation），通过文化适应、目标文本改写、修订和条件性视觉调整四个专门智能体的协调合作，解决文化知识理解、意图与语气保留、多模态一致性三大核心挑战，在中英文双向迷因创译中显著优于所有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络迷因是跨语言、跨文化交流中的普遍多模态沟通形式，但现有翻译或文本改写方法无法直接应用于迷因改编，因为迷因创译需要同时保留交际意图、适应文化特定含义并维持图文一致性。目前缺乏针对这一任务的显式分析和专用框架。

Method: 作者首先对跨文化迷因创译进行了显式的任务分析，识别出三大核心挑战：文化特定知识理解、意图与语气保留、多模态一致性。基于该分析，提出了一个多智能体框架，包含四个协调的专门智能体：文化适应智能体、目标文本改写智能体、修订智能体和条件性视觉调整智能体。框架通过协调反馈强化目标文本适应能力，以处理需要更深层次文化或视觉干预的困难案例。

Result: 在双向中英文迷因创译上使用人工评估和LLM-as-a-Judge两种方式评估，方法在所有基线上均一致胜出。人工评估中，在四个维度上均取得最佳表现，比最强基线平均提升33.1%；LLM-as-a-Judge中，取得最高Top-1排名率（60%，第二好基线为26%）。消融分析表明每个组件均有贡献。错误分析显示剩余瓶颈在于幽默重构和图文对齐，而非简单的文化知识缺口。

Conclusion: 多智能体框架能有效解决跨文化迷因创译中的核心挑战，且各组件具有独立贡献。未来工作应聚焦于幽默迁移，以解决当前框架在幽默重构和图文对齐方面的局限。

Abstract: Internet memes are a pervasive form of multimodal online communication; however, such communication often involves users from diverse linguistic and cultural backgrounds. Therefore, adapting memes across cultures and languages is a central challenge for enabling mutual understanding in online communication. Unlike ordinary translation or standalone text rewriting, cross-cultural meme transcreation must jointly preserve communicative intent, adapt culture-dependent meaning for the target audience, and maintain coherence between text and image. In this work, we first provide an explicit task analysis of cross-cultural meme transcreation and identify three core challenges: culture-specific knowledge understanding, intent and tone preservation, and multimodal consistency. Based on this analysis, we propose a multi-agent framework with specialized agents that are coordinated to address these challenges through cultural adaptation, target text rewriting, revision, and conditional visual adjustment. The framework strengthens target text adaptation with coordinated feedback to handle difficult cases that require deeper cultural or visual intervention. We evaluate the framework on bidirectional Chinese-English meme transcreation using both human evaluation and LLM-as-a-Judge. Our method consistently outperforms all baselines across both evaluation settings. In human evaluation, it achieves the best performance on all four dimensions and delivers a 33.1% average improvement over the strongest baseline, while in LLM-as-a-Judge, it attains the highest Top-1 ranking rate (60% versus 26% for the second-best baseline). Further analysis indicates that each component contributes to the performance. Our error analysis suggests that the remaining bottlenecks lie in humor reconstruction and image-text alignment rather than simple cultural knowledge gaps, pointing to future work on humor transfer.

</details>


### [174] [GRAIN: Bridging Name and Narrative Shifts in Real-World Graph Reasoning through Invariance-Rewarded Agentic RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27142)
*Zike Yuan,Han Zhang,Jianzhi Yan,Le Liu,Cai Ke,Huozhi Zhou,Jian Xie,Jiran Yin,Yukun Cao,Yue Yu,Hui Wang,Ming Liu,Bing Qin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出GRAIN，一个基于强化学习的单智能体框架，通过结构不变性奖励（Structure Invariance Reward）将LLM的推理建模为语义解析和工具执行流水线，以解决LLM在图任务中对节点标识符和任务表述变化的脆弱性问题。同时引入GRIT基准测试，GRAIN在准确率上超越多智能体基线16.45%，延迟降低约24%，并将OOD差距从15.77%减半至7.80%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在标准化图任务中表现良好，但对真实世界中的节点标识符变化和任务表述变化非常脆弱；确定性的图工具对这类变化具有不变性，但从嘈杂文本中提取拓扑结构对LLM而言高度不稳定，容易过拟合表面模式；而通过多智能体系统缓解解析失败又会带来过高的延迟开销。

Method: GRAIN是一个单智能体框架，通过强化学习优化。它将推理过程建模为语义解析和工具执行的流水线，并由结构不变性奖励（Structure Invariance Reward）引导。该奖励通过验证提取的中间图与真实拓扑结构的一致性，迫使LLM学习鲁棒的文本到结构映射，而非记忆语言表面伪影。同时提出GRIT基准，用于评估模型对语言变化（linguistic shifts）的敏感性。

Result: GRAIN在准确率上比多智能体基线高出16.45%，延迟降低约24%；在结构泛化方面表现优越，将SFT模型的OOD差距从15.77%降至7.80%（差距减半），并在超出训练分布的大规模图上保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: GRAIN证明了通过强化学习结合结构不变性奖励，单智能体框架能够在保持低延迟的同时显著提升LLM在图任务中对语言变化的鲁棒性，并且具有良好的结构泛化能力。

Abstract: Despite their potential in standardized graph tasks, Large Language Models (LLMs) remain brittle to real-world shifts in node identifiers and task formulation. While deterministic graph tools are invariant to such shifts, extracting topological structures from noisy text is highly fragile for LLMs, which often overfit to surface patterns. Moreover, mitigating these parsing failures via multi-agent systems incurs prohibitive latency. To address this, we propose GRAIN, a single-agent framework optimized via reinforcement learning. GRAIN models reasoning as a semantic parsing and tool-execution pipeline, guided by a Structure Invariance Reward. By validating extracted intermediate graphs against ground-truth topologies, this reward forces the LLM to learn robust text-to-structure mappings rather than memorizing linguistic artifacts. We also introduce GRIT, a benchmark evaluating sensitivity to such linguistic shifts. GRAIN outperforms multi-agent baselines by 16.45\% in accuracy with approximately 24\% lower latency. Furthermore, it demonstrates superior structural generalization, halving the out-of-distribution (OOD) gap of SFT models (from 15.77\% to 7.80\%) and maintaining robustness on large-scale graphs beyond the training distribution.

</details>


### [175] [Feature Transformation Enhanced Jacobi Polynomial Graph Filtering for Graph Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27144)
*Xiang Wang,Zhijun Cheng,Zhenyu Meng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出JPGFN（特征变换增强的雅可比多项式图滤波网络）用于图异常检测，通过特征分离变换网络学习细粒度节点特征、基于雅可比多项式的自适应图滤波模块捕捉复杂频域特征，以及节点标签约束模块利用标签信息，在多个真实数据集上显著优于主流方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于频域滤波的图异常检测方法面临三大挑战：1）使用静态基函数构造图滤波器，无法自适应数据频域分布；2）未充分考虑节点特征向量中各属性的重要性，丢失细粒度信息；3）对节点标签的利用不充分。

Method: 提出JPGFN方法，包含三个核心模块：1）FSTNN（特征分离变换网络），通过特征分离及对不同维度节点特征施加非线性变换来学习细粒度节点特征；2）基于雅可比多项式的自适应图滤波模块，自适应捕获图信号的复杂频域特征；3）节点标签约束模块，促进节点标签的利用以增强检测性能。

Result: 在多个真实世界数据集上的实验结果表明，该方法显著优于主流基线方法。

Conclusion: JPGFN通过结合自适应图滤波、细粒度特征学习和标签约束，有效解决了现有频域滤波GAD方法在滤波器适应性、特征细粒度信息以及标签利用方面的不足，验证了各模块在提升图异常检测性能方面的有效性。

Abstract: In recent years, graph anomaly detection (GAD) based on frequency-domain filtering have achieved promising results. However, existing approaches still face three major challenges: First, they use static basic function to constructed graph filter which cannot effectively adapt to the frequency-domain distribution of graph data. Second, they fail to adequately consider the importance information of each attribute in the node feature vector, leading to the loss of fine-grained information. Third, they insufficiently utilize node labels for GAD. To address these issues, this paper proposes a novel graph anomaly detection method called JPGFN (Feature Transformation Enhanced Jacobi Polynomial Graph Filtering Network). First, a Feature Separation Transformation Network (FSTNN) is developed to better learn fine-grained node features by feature separation and applying nonlinear transformations to node features across different dimensions. Second, an adaptive Jacobi polynomial graph filtering module is constructed based on Jacobi polynomials to adaptively capture complex frequency-domain features of graph signals. Finally, a node label constraint module is developed to facilitate the use of node labels and enhance the performance of GAD. Experimental results on multiple real-world datasets demonstrate that the proposed method significantly outperforms mainstream approaches.

</details>


### [176] [Thomson: Continual Learning of Frontier Models for SovereignAI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27147)
*Shengzhuang Chen,Jerrod Parker,Yejin Bang,Andrew M. Bean,Nabeel Seedat,Stefan Winzeck,Daniil Glazko,Jannik Zgraggen,Fangyi Yu,Scott Arnott,Dietrich Trautmann,Luca Ciuffreda,Guglielmo Bonifazi,Davide Romano,Bradley Bell,Kirsty Fielding,Daniele Giofrè,Tom Zielund,Ipshita Chatterjee,Sneha Murthy Ghantasala,Manpreet Nanreh,John Scoville,Maciej Sakowicz,Wassim Seifeddine,Lukas Thede,Jonathan Richard Schwarz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出通过持续学习（Continual Learning）在开放权重模型上实现前沿性能，使广大机构能够以远低于预期的计算和人力预算独立构建、部署和治理AI（SovereignAI），并以Thomson模型验证了该方法在不同任务上的竞争性表现，同时几乎完全消除了灾难性遗忘问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿模型开发被认为只属于少数资金充裕的玩家，造成开发者与用户之间的信息、经济和权力不对称。虽然已有关于SovereignAI的讨论，但缺乏在不同资金条件下短期实现的具体建议。

Method: 利用现代中期与后期训练技术栈（mid- & post-training stack），在开放权重模型上实施持续学习，在每一阶段引入保持可塑性与稳定性的防护措施，对参数执行最小数量的高影响力干预，而非小规模微调、提示工程或冻结模型的工具增强等受限方法。

Result: 训练的Thomson模型在多代模型增量之间表现出可比拟的性能提升，在智能体任务、安全性、法律、税务、多语言及大规模深度研究上表现具有竞争力。评估呈现独特的π形模式：广泛能力（包括未显式目标的能力）均有显著改进，且几乎完全消除了窄域适配中常见的遗忘问题。

Conclusion: 前沿性能可被广泛的机构获得，通过持续学习策略使大多数SovereignAI技术栈（模型、工具基础设施、价值观与数据隐私）的所有权对更多参与者可行，打破了少数资金充裕玩家对前沿模型开发的垄断。

Abstract: The development of frontier models is commonly perceived to be the exclusive remit of a small number of heavily funded players, creating an information, economic and power asymmetry between developers and the diverse user base of modern AI. Recent public discourse acknowledges this concern, calling for SovereignAI (an organisation's capability to independently build, deploy and govern AI use), but offers little concrete advice on how this can be achieved in the short term under a diversity of funding settings. We argue that frontier performance is achievable by a wide range of institutions through Continual Learning on readily available open-weight models. Unlike limited approaches such as small-scale fine-tuning, prompt engineering, or tool-augmentation of a frozen model, our approach exploits a modern mid- & post-training stack while introducing safeguards that preserve both plasticity and stability at each stage, making the minimal number of high-impact interventions on the parameters. This yields gains comparable to those typically seen across multiple successive model generations, at compute and personnel budgets substantially lower than commonly thought, making ownership of large parts of the SovereignAI stack (model, tool infrastructure, values & data privacy) viable for far more actors. We demonstrate this with Thomson, a general-purpose frontier model trained with an enhanced focus on high-stakes professional work. Thomson performs competitively with recent frontier models across agentic tasks, safety, legal, tax & multilingualism, and large-scale Deep Research. Evaluations show a distinctive $π$-shaped pattern: distinct improvements across a wide range of capabilities, including those not explicitly targeted, while almost completely eliminating the forgetting problem common to narrow domain adaptation.

</details>


### [177] [BPMN4CAI: A BPMN Extension for Modeling Dynamic Conversational AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27149)
*Björn-Lennart Eger,Daniel Rose,Barbara Dinter*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出BPMN4CAI，一种标准合规的BPMN扩展框架，采用设计科学研究方法论，用于表示业务流程中的对话式AI交互，并通过案例研究验证其适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对话式AI系统（如聊天机器人和虚拟助手）在数字业务流程中日益重要，但现有BPMN标准在表示动态、上下文敏感交互时存在局限性，存在方法论和实践层面的研究空白。

Method: 采用设计科学研究（DSR）方法论，系统性地扩展现有BPMN元素并引入专门组件，开发标准合规的BPMN4CAI扩展框架。

Result: 通过案例研究证明BPMN4CAI框架能够促进自适应决策过程、稳健的上下文管理以及业务流程中对话式AI的透明交互。

Conclusion: BPMN4CAI扩展有效弥补了BPMN标准在对话式AI建模方面的不足，为业务流程中集成对话式AI提供了可行的建模解决方案。

Abstract: Conversational AI systems, such as chatbots and virtual assistants, are becoming increasingly important to digital business processes. However, the established Business Process Model and Notation (BPMN) standard faces challenges when representing dynamic, context-sensitive interactions. This paper addresses this methodological and practical research gap by developing a standard-compliant BPMN extension (BPMN4CAI). Using Design Science Research methodology, this paper develops an approach that systematically extends existing BPMN elements and incorporates specialized components. The applicability and relevance of the BPMN4CAI framework are demonstrated and evaluated through a case study. The results show that the BPMN4CAI extension facilitates adaptive decision-making processes, robust context management, and transparent interactions for Conversational AI within business processes.

</details>


### [178] [Calibrated Enough to Know, Not Calibrated to Act: Fabricated Evidence Makes LLM Agents Commit to the Unknowable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27167)
*Pranav Aggarwal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现，LLM智能体在面对专业外观的市场面板时，对不可预测的问题做出方向性判断的概率（54.0%）远高于仅被直接提问时（6.5%），且即使面板数据完全捏造，承诺率与真实数据无统计学差异。问题不在于模型能力不足或信念缺失，而在于"行动/不行动"的决策门控环节失效。通过对3B模型在540个合成案例上进行监督微调，可将承诺率降至0%，并能泛化到未见领域，但这种门控具有上下文脆弱性——当响应格式剥夺推理空间时即失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究LLM智能体在面临不可预测问题时的过度自信现象，识别"权威包装"（如专业市场面板）如何影响模型的决定，以及这种失败的根源结构。

Method: 对12个前沿模型进行对照实验，逐步升级证据展示（裸问题→专业面板→完全捏造的数据面板），测量模型做出方向性判断的比率；同时测试模型对问题的可知晓性分类能力、概率判断准确性；最后对3B模型进行540个合成案例（骰子、硬币、罐子、计时器等）的监督微调训练行动门控。

Result: 承诺率从6.5%升至54.0%；捏造面板与真实面板产生的承诺率（36.8% vs 37.6%）在统计上不可区分。模型回答可回答问题的准确率近乎完美，但判断问题可知晓性的能力（90%识别为不可约）与实际行动严重脱节（仅有0.4%相应不行动）。3B模型微调后将承诺率降至0.0%并在三个未见领域泛化，但在严格响应格式下门控失效。

Conclusion: LLM过度承诺的根源不是能力或信念问题，而是"行动/不行动"的门控机制失败。该门控可被训练但也具有上下文敏感性——在保留推理空间的格式中门控有效，在刚性格式中则崩溃。部署时需要同时认识这两个特性。

Abstract: An LLM agent shown a professional-looking market panel commits to a directional call on a provably unpredictable question far more often than one asked the bare question: across 12 frontier models, commitment rises from 6.5% to 54.0% as evidence is escalated. It commits just as readily when every number on the panel is invented: fabricating the entire display, so nothing the model can see is true except the question itself, still lifts commitment from 24.5% to 36.8%, statistically indistinguishable from the 37.6% produced by genuine market data. What unlocks confident action is not information but the authority of its packaging. The failure is narrow and locatable. Incapacity is not the answer: on matched answerable questions attached to the same panels, the same models answer essentially always, at near-perfect accuracy. Nor is it belief - stated probabilities barely move across the gradient that swings action by 48 points, and score worse than a climatological baseline. Missing judgment isn't it either: asked to classify a question's knowability before acting, models call it irreducible 90% of the time and then commit on just 0.4% of those. The act/don't-act gate is what fails, and the effect is concentrated in a few models rather than universal. Because the gate is separable, it can be trained. Supervised fine-tuning of a 3B model on 540 synthetic cases, predominantly dice, coins, jars and timers, drives commitment to 0.0% on the original cases and transfers to three unseen domains. It does not survive everything: the gate holds exactly when the response format leaves room to reason, and rigid formats that remove that room leave the model confident and wrong on questions it otherwise answers correctly. The gate is trainable and context-fragile, and deployment needs both halves of that sentence.

</details>


### [179] [What Makes Good Agentic Data? An ACE Lens on Data Generation for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27260)
*Xingshan Zeng,Zishan Xu,Boju Zhang,Yuzhou Wu,Lingzhi Wang,Jianghao Lin,Liangyou Li,Yasheng Wang,Lifeng Shang,Xin Jiang,Weinan Zhang,Yong Yu,Qun Liu,Weiwen Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个两层级框架来统一理解智能体数据生成领域：首先将智能体数据表示为因子化对象(E,q,τ,v)，按主要锚点和依赖结构组织生成范式；其次通过Accuracy-Complexity-divErsity（ACE）视角将生成形式化为约束分布设计。核心观点是智能体数据生成的关键不在于产生更多数据，而在于随着智能体和环境演化持续分配有效、有信息量且非冗余的经验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体日益依赖生成式交互数据来学习与外部环境交互，但现有工作按领域分散组织，评估方式异构，导致难以识别共同的生成机制，同时常将候选构建与验证和选择混为一谈，缺乏统一的框架来系统理解该领域。

Method: 提出两层级框架：(1) 数据表示层——将智能体数据表示为因子化对象(E,q,τ,v)，包含环境规范、任务信号、交互实现和可选验证器，并按主要锚点和依赖结构组织生成范式；(2) 约束设计层——通过ACE视角（准确性建立可行支持域，复杂性相对于声明的学习者和执行配置放置学习质量，多样性控制覆盖度和冗余度）将生成形式化为约束分布设计。

Result: 通过框架审视已有工作，发现文献呈现向执行接地的准确性、学习者相对的复杂性以及超越表面变化或数据集规模的多样性的转变趋势，并基于ACE视角探讨了扩展、数据来源、训练制度和自适应学习等方向的启示。

Conclusion: 智能体数据生成的核心挑战不是简单的数据量扩展，而是在智能体和环境持续演化的过程中，如何持续分配有效、有信息量且非冗余的经验数据。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly rely on generated interaction data to learn how to interact with external environments. Agentic data generation must maintain consistency among environments, tasks, interactions, and success signals while producing experience that is useful rather than merely abundant. Existing work spans many agent domains, but domain-centered organization and heterogeneous evaluation often obscure common generation mechanisms and conflate candidate construction with verification and selection. This work develops a two-level framework for the field. First, we represent agentic data as a common factorized object $(E,q,τ,v)$, comprising an environment specification, task signal, interaction realization, and optional verifier. We organize generation paradigms by their primary anchor and dependency structure. Second, we formulate generation as constrained distribution design through the Accuracy-Complexity-divErsity (ACE) lens. Accuracy establishes the feasible support of grounded and internally consistent data. Within this support, Complexity places learning mass relative to the capability of a declared learner and execution configuration, while divErsity controls coverage and redundancy of data. Using this framework, we explore how prior work verifies generated experience, constructs and calibrates difficulty, and expands behavioral coverage. The literature reveals a shift toward execution-grounded accuracy, learner-relative complexity, and diversity beyond surface variation or dataset size. We further discuss broader directions and emerging trends in agentic data generation through the ACE lens, including their implications for scaling, data sources, training regimes and adaptive learning. Overall, the central challenge is not simply to generate more data, but to continually allocate valid, informative, and non-redundant experience as agents and environments evolve.

</details>


### [180] [LLMs Can Design Near-Optimal OR Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27296)
*Jackie Baek*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究大型语言模型（LLM）能否为规格明确的操作研究（OR）问题设计有效算法。研究发现最强的模型gpt-5.6-sol在几乎所有测试实例上与最佳现有方法相当或更优，即使在仅给出问题类描述和参数范围、不预看测试实例的"二级"设定下也是如此，表明前沿LLM可以成为OR算法设计的有力实证基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索LLM作为一个严肃的经验基线用于自动化算法设计，特别是在规格明确的OR问题（库存控制、排队网络控制和品类优化）中，评估其能否超越传统的专业化求解方法。

Method: 在三个OR问题（库存控制、排队网络控制、品类优化）上，以两个层级评估LLM能力：(1) 第一层级：模型接收单个问题实例并返回该实例的解；(2) 第二层级：模型仅接收问题类描述和宽泛参数范围，返回一个能将实例参数映射到解的算法。采用单一未调优的提示词描述问题，模型使用带固定计算预算的Python沙盒工具，人工干预最小化。

Result: 最强模型gpt-5.6-sol在几乎所有评估实例上达到或超越最佳现有方法，包括在第二层级（算法在预看评估实例前就已固定）的设定下表现依然出色。此外，相隔不到八个月发布的模型之间性能显著提升，表明该能力正在快速演进。

Conclusion: 对于规格明确的OR问题，单次未调优的LLM查询已能产生与专业化方法竞争甚至更优的算法。前沿LLM应被视为OR算法设计的严肃实证基线，且这一能力仍在快速进步中。

Abstract: We ask whether large language models (LLMs) can design effective algorithms for well-specified operations research (OR) problems. We study inventory control, queueing network control, and assortment optimization. We evaluate two levels of LLM use: at level 1, the model receives one problem instance and returns a solution for that instance; at level 2, it receives only the problem class description and broad parameter ranges, and returns an algorithm that maps instance parameters to solutions. Human input is minimal: we give one untuned prompt that describes the problem, and the model has access to a Python sandbox tool with a fixed compute budget.
  The strongest model we test, gpt-5.6-sol, matches or outperforms the best existing method on almost all evaluated instances. This holds even at level 2, where the returned algorithm is fixed before seeing the evaluation instances. Performance also improves sharply across models released less than eight months apart, suggesting that this capability is moving quickly. Thus, for the well-specified operations problems we study, a single untuned LLM query can already produce algorithms competitive with specialized methods. These results suggest that frontier LLMs can be a serious empirical baseline for algorithm design in well-specified OR problems.

</details>


### [181] [Naive Prompt Optimization: Rethinking the Need for Complex Prompt Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27266)
*Yuan Chang,Xiaoqi Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出NPO（朴素提示优化），一种轻量级单谱系迭代提示优化方法，利用教师模型和rollout反馈来迭代修正提示。实验表明NPO在更少的rollout次数下即可达到或超越复杂的GEPA方法，且教师模型越强优势越明显；在交互式游戏中与GEPA表现大体相当，优化后的提示还可迁移到同家族其他学生模型上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提示优化被认为是一种能够在降低计算成本的同时达到与模型微调相当性能的方法，对加速智能体递归自我改进至关重要。然而，近期研究趋势倾向于使用不必要的复杂提示优化器，作者希望探索简单方法是否也能达到或超越复杂方法的性能。

Method: NPO是一种单谱系（single-lineage）轻量级方法，使用教师模型生成提示修正，并通过rollout反馈（策略评估结果）来指导迭代优化过程，无需维护多个并行优化轨迹或复杂的搜索策略。

Result: NPO在更少的rollout次数下达到或优于GEPA；教师模型越强，NPO相对GEPA的优势越明显；在交互式游戏中与GEPA大体相当，但GRPO在某些不适合提示优化的任务上表现更好；NPO优化的提示可原样应用到其他学生模型并带来相似的性能提升，尤其在同模型家族内部迁移效果显著。

Conclusion: 初步结果表明，简单、线性的提示优化方法可以媲美甚至超越显著更复杂和精细化的搜索过程，提示优化器的搜索复杂度部分可由更强的教师推理能力所替代。

Abstract: Efficiently improving autonomous agents across diverse tasks is central to accelerating recursive self-improvement (RSI) in agentic AI, with prompt optimization emerging as a promising approach capable of delivering performance gains comparable to those achieved by fine-tuning model weights, while reducing computational costs in both optimization and serving. However, recent developments increasingly favor unnecessarily complex prompt optimizers. We introduce Naive Prompt Optimization (NPO), a lightweight single-lineage method that iteratively revises prompts using a teacher model with rollout feedback. NPO achieves comparable or better performance than GEPA with fewer rollouts, and its advantage increases with stronger teacher models, suggesting that stronger teacher reasoning can partially substitute for optimizer-side search complexity. In interactive games, NPO remains broadly competitive with GEPA, while GRPO performs better on some tasks less amenable to prompt optimization. We also show that NPO-optimized prompts elicit similar performance improvements when applied verbatim to other student models, especially across models within the same family. Overall, our preliminary results show that simple, linear prompt optimization can rival substantially more sophisticated and complex search procedures.

</details>


### [182] [BrailleBench: Investigating Multi-Criteria Braille Comprehension in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27268)
*Jinghan Zhang,Fengran Mo,Zhiyu Chen,Xiaoyan Han,Kunpeng Liu,Chang-Tien Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了BrailleBench基准，用于评估大语言模型（LLM）在盲文理解方面的能力。该基准包含5,570个实例，覆盖数学、常识和多跳问答，涵盖英文及Braille 1级和2级。评估六个代表性LLM后发现，模型在印刷英文与盲文可访问性之间存在持续差距，盲文理解和表达不对称，2级盲文在输入侧尤其脆弱。所有资源公开可用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM在知识和计算辅助方面具备强大能力，但这些能力应同样惠及弱势群体。然而，现有AI系统对盲人和聋盲用户是否足够包容、能否通过盲文访问同等功能尚不清楚。盲文的指示符、缩写和数字表示对模型理解提出了独特要求，但鲜有研究系统评估LLM的盲文能力。

Method: 作者构建了BrailleBench基准，通过确定性、专家审核的流水线和自建Braille Toolkit生成数据（不依赖LLM生成实例），从五个数据集中对齐5,570个实例。设计了多种配置以评估系统理解盲文内容、以盲文表达答案以及完成端到端盲文交互的能力，并评估六个代表性LLM。

Result: 评估结果显示：印刷英文能力与盲文可访问性之间存在持续性差距；盲文理解与表达呈现不对称性；2级盲文在输入侧比1级盲文更加脆弱；完全盲文请求进一步降低模型性能。

Conclusion: 实验结果揭示了当前LLM在盲文可访问性方面的显著不足，为未来盲文AI系统的开发提供了有价值的指导。BrailleBench及其相关资源已公开，供后续研究使用。

Abstract: Although Large language models (LLMs) mediate access to knowledge and computational assistance, their capabilities should benefit vulnerable groups in the same way. However, it is unclear whether existing AI systems are inclusive enough for blind and deafblind users to access the same functionality through Braille, whose indicators, contractions, and digital representations introduce distinct requirements for model comprehension. To this end, we introduce BrailleBench, a benchmark for evaluating LLMs in Braille comprehension from different Criteria. BrailleBench aligns 5,570 instances from five datasets, including mathematics, commonsense, and multi-hop question answering across English and Braille Grades 1 and 2. Different configurations are designed to understand whether the systems can comprehend Braille-authored content, express answers in Braille, and complete end-to-end Braille interaction. To ensure the quality and prevent evaluation bias, the benchmark is built through a deterministic, expert-reviewed pipeline via a self-created Braille Toolkit without using any data instances generated by LLMs. We evaluate six representative LLMs from various aspects. The results reveal a persistent gap between print-English capability and Braille accessibility. Braille understanding and expression are asymmetric, where Grade 2 is especially fragile on the input side compared to Grade 1, and fully Braille requests further reduce performance. The experimental observations provide valuable guidance for the development of future Braille AI systems. All related resources in BrailleBench are publicly available for future research.

</details>


### [183] [Learning a Continuous Sepsis Severity Score Without Hour-by-Hour Supervision: A Two-Site Retrospective Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27421)
*Kevin Zhu,Ryan Zhang,Baraa Abed,Tilendra Choudhary,Malvern Madondo,Mehak Arora,Yixuan Yang,Alasdair Gent,Aditya Nagori,Omer T. Inan,Krista L. Haines,Patrick Georgoff,Suresh M. Agarwal,Vijay Krishnamoorthy,Tetsu Ohnuma,Mihai V. Podgoreanu,Michael R. Pinsky,Gilles Clermont,Craig M. Coopersmith,Craig S. Jabaley,Rishikesan Kamaleswaran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文开发了一种基于患者轨迹学习的脓毒症指数，利用43个常规记录变量在72小时治疗窗口内进行死亡率排名学习，取代了传统固定权重和离散化的脓毒症严重程度评分。在两个医院系统的29,116和7,691名Sepsis-3患者中进行回顾性两队列验证，结果显示非幸存者比幸存者得分高1.19-1.64分（0-10分制），与乳酸变化相关性良好（rho=0.39），跨机构一致性为70-77%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的脓毒症严重程度指标（如SOFA）基于数十年前固定的变量和权重建立，离散化粗糙，校准队列已不符合当代重症医学现状。目前没有从患者轨迹直接学习的替代方案在常规临床中使用，需要一种能够反映现代脓毒症病程动态变化的预后指标。

Method: 采用回顾性双队列研究设计，在两家医院系统（马萨诸塞州和乔治亚州）共36,807名符合Sepsis-3标准的成人患者中，使用43个常规记录变量在72小时治疗窗口开发脓毒症指数。创新点在于将死亡率作为治疗层面的排名信号而非逐状态目标，允许信用在时间步上非均匀再分配。采用20%永久测试集进行评估，通过临床小故事和Spearman相关性验证，使用bootstrap重采样整患者获取不确定性区间。

Result: 在该排名方案下，非幸存者在所有基线SOFA-2分层内得分比幸存者高1.19-1.64分，在乳酸、平均动脉压和肌酐分层内也有类似结果。患者内指数变化与乳酸变化相关（Spearman rho=0.39，n=1,854），MAP和肌酐的相关性较弱。跨机构一致性为同机构相关性的70-77%。外部患者内相关性为0.54和0.59，对应上限0.92和0.90。指数与既有指标相关，而零对照接近零。

Conclusion: 该指数展示了每小时的预后信息，能够有意义地区分患者结局且符合临床预期，表明其作为补充临床判断的决策支持工具具有潜力。

Abstract: Currently used sepsis severity indices rely on fixed variables and weights established decades ago, which are coarsely discretized and calibrated to a cohort that no longer reflects contemporary critical care. No alternative learned directly from patient trajectories is in routine use. We conducted a retrospective two-cohort study on a total of 29,116 and 7,691 adult patients meeting Sepsis-3 criteria from two hospital systems in Massachusetts and Georgie, respectively.
  We developed a sepsis index using 43 routinely charted variables over a 72-hour treatment window. Unlike previous studies, we use mortality as a treatment-level ranking signal rather than a per-state target, allowing credit to be redistributed non-uniformly across timesteps. Evaluation was done on a permanent 20% test holdout, using clinical vignettes and Spearman correlation. Uncertainty intervals were obtained by bootstrap resampling of whole patients. Under this ranking scheme, non-survivors scored 1.19-1.64 points higher than survivors on a 0-10 scale within all strata of baseline SOFA-2, with similar results stratifying within lactate, mean arterial pressure (MAP), and creatinine. Within-patient change in the index correlated with change in lactate (Spearman rho = 0.39; n = 1,854). Similar, weaker correlations were found for MAP and creatinine. On a cohort level, cross-institutional agreement measured by Spearman correlation between models trained on different sites, were 70-77% of same-site correlation. External within-patient correlations were 0.54 and 0.59 against ceilings of 0.92 and 0.90. Our index also correlated with established indices, while null controls stayed near zero.
  Our index demonstrated hourly prognostic information that meaningfully separates patient outcomes and is consistent with clinical expectation, indicating potential as a decision support tool complementing clinical judgement.

</details>


### [184] [Verify Smarter, Evolve Further: Efficient Harness Evolution through Behavior-Aware Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27311)
*Jinghan Xu,Yikai Zhang,Aili Chen,Weiyuan Li,Jiaqing Liang,Deqing Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HarnessLens是一个预算感知的自动化智能体（agent）框架演化框架，通过联合探索任务空间与可配置组件、从执行轨迹中派生候选修改，并利用可归因证据门控在行为相关任务上选择性验证候选方案，从而在更低评估预算下实现更可靠的harness演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的propose-and-verify方法通常在固定任务集上对所有候选进行评分，这会造成两个问题：一是在无关行为上浪费大量rollout资源；二是聚合分数会掩盖特定的性能回归。由于harness（智能体外壳）的适应需要昂贵的验证成本，因此需要一种更高效且更具可追溯性的验证机制。

Method: HarnessLens框架包含三个关键设计：(1) 联合探索任务空间与用户可配置的用户组件；(2) 从执行轨迹中推导候选修改方案；(3) 通过可归因证据门控（attributable-evidence gate）在行为相关任务上选择性验证候选方案，避免在无关行为上浪费评估预算。

Result: 在三个智能体harness和四个基准测试上，HarnessLens将平均保留（held-out）性能提升了7.6%-13.6%，同时相比竞争基线消耗了更少的评估预算。

Conclusion: 行为感知的验证配合显式归因机制，能够在受约束的交互预算下实现更可靠且样本效率更高的harness演化。

Abstract: Agent harnesses shape how language-model agents use instructions, tools, and runtime components, but adapting these harnesses requires costly verification. Existing propose-and-verify methods typically score every candidate on a fixed task set, wasting rollouts on unrelated behaviors and allowing aggregate scores to obscure specific regressions. We introduce HarnessLens, a budget-aware framework for automated harness evolution. HarnessLens jointly explores the task space and user-configurable components, derives candidate modifications from execution trajectories, and selectively verifies each candidate on behavior-relevant tasks using an attributable-evidence gate. Across three agent harnesses and four benchmarks, HarnessLens improves average held-out performance by 7.6-13.6% while consuming substantially less evaluation budget than competing baselines. These results demonstrate that behavior-aware verification with explicit attribution enables more reliable and sample-efficient harness evolution under constrained interaction budgets. Our code is available at https://github.com/jhxu5214/HarnessLens.

</details>


### [185] [Not All Eval-Awareness Is Equal: Capabilities Framing Predicts Compliance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27340)
*Allison Zhuang,Santiago Aranguri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文挑战了安全评估管线中将"评估意识"（eval-awareness）视为单一可抑制量的传统做法，通过在Qwen3-32B和FORTRESS数据集上实验，证明评估意识在思维链中可被细分为能力导向和安全导向等不同框架，这些框架对模型遵从行为的预测差异巨大（能力框架比安全框架高出24-46个百分点），并通过CoT预填充干预验证了因果性，最终指出评估意识并非行为上均匀的单一概念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的安全评估管线将评估意识视为单一量进行抑制，但这种方法可能掩盖评估意识内部异质性，导致对安全相关行为的理解不准确或控制失效。作者旨在揭示评估意识的不同语义框架及其对模型遵从性预测的显著差异。

Method: 在Qwen3-32B模型上使用FORTRESS数据集，通过分析思维链（chain-of-thought）中言语化的评估意识，将其分类为能力导向框架、安全导向框架、两者兼具或两者皆非；使用CoT预填充（prefill）干预对评估意识为负的rollouts进行因果验证；对比不同steering条件下各框架的遵从率差异。

Result: 能力框架在预测遵从性上比安全框架高出24至46个百分点（涵盖所有测试的steering条件）；11个预填充干预中有10个使遵从性沿预测方向移动，支持因果关系；聚合抑制率的变化可能掩盖安全相关成分的未变化，相同的"X%抑制评估意识"可能对应质上不同的行为结果。

Conclusion: 评估意识不是行为上均匀的统一概念，安全评估管线的"评估意识抑制"操作需要区分其语义子类型；仅关注聚合抑制率可能产生误导性的安全评估结论。

Abstract: Steering interventions targeting eval-awareness, a model's recognition that it is being tested, are increasingly used in safety evaluation pipelines, where evaluation-awareness is treated as a single quantity to be suppressed. We show that verbalized eval-awareness in chain-of-thought can be identified as capabilities-flavored ("the user is testing my ability to follow instructions"), safety-flavored ("the user is testing my boundaries"), both, or neither: framings that predict compliance very differently. On Qwen3-32B over the FORTRESS dataset, capabilities-framing predicts compliance with a +24 to +46 percentage-point gap over safety-framing across all tested steering conditions. A CoT-prefill intervention on eval-awareness-negative rollouts suggests the link is causal, with 10 of 11 prefills shifting compliance in the predicted direction. Then, eval-awareness is not behaviorally uniform: aggregate suppression rates can move while the safety-relevant component does not, and the same "X% suppression of eval-awareness" can correspond to qualitatively different behavioral outcomes.

</details>


### [186] [Sophistication in GenAI Use: Field Evidence from a Large Firm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27364)
*Nicholas J. Hallman,Zachary T. Kowaleski,Anu Puvvada,Jaime J. Schmidt*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文利用某大型企业8个月内近4000名后台员工的71万余条提示词及大模型响应的专有数据，研究了生成式AI使用娴熟度的分布情况。研究发现：资深员工表现出更高的AI使用娴熟度（印证领域专长与AI能力互补）；娴熟度在不同职能部门间差异显著，在战略、数字创新和项目管理等部门最高；娴熟度随时间没有改善，正式AI培训也未带来持久提升，表明娴熟使用难以改变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究对生成式AI在组织中的实际使用方式缺乏深入了解，尤其缺少对员工AI使用娴熟度（sophistication）的大规模实证刻画。作者旨在利用企业内部的专有数据，系统度量并解释后台员工在使用生成式AI时表现出的娴熟度差异，为管理者改进AI应用效果及研究者开展后续研究提供基础。

Method: 使用某大型企业2025年8个月间的专有数据，涵盖约4000名后台员工、分属15个职能领域的713,564条员工提示词及对应的大语言模型响应。通过分析提示词的复杂程度来衡量AI使用娴熟度，并比较不同职级、职能领域以及随时间/培训前后的差异。

Result: 三项主要发现：(1) 资深员工的生成式AI使用娴熟度更高，与领域专长补充AI能力的观点一致；(2) 娴熟度在职能领域间差异显著，在战略、数字创新和项目管理这三个共同关注公司级战略举措与组织变革的领域最高；(3) 娴熟度既未随时间改善，正式AI培训后也未出现持久提升，表明娴熟的使用方式可能难以通过现有手段改变。

Conclusion: 研究提供了对生成式AI娴熟使用方式的度量与洞察，可帮助管理者改善AI应用结果，并为未来研究提供基础。娴熟度受职级和职能背景影响显著，且难以通过时间或正式培训自发提升，提示需要在培训设计和使用策略上采取更有针对性的干预。

Abstract: We study how sophistication in generative AI (genAI) use varies among the back-office workforce of a large firm. Using proprietary data, we observe 713,564 employee prompts and their corresponding large language model responses from nearly 4,000 back-office employees across 15 functional areas over eight months in 2025. We document three main findings. First, senior employees exhibit more sophisticated genAI use, consistent with domain expertise complementing genAI capabilities. Second, sophistication varies considerably across functions and is highest in Strategy, Digital Innovation, and Project Management, three groups that share a focus on firmwide strategic initiatives and organizational change. Third, we observe neither improvements in sophistication over time nor lasting improvements following formal AI training, suggesting that sophisticated use can be difficult to change. Together, our study provides measures of and insights into sophisticated genAI use that managers can use to improve outcomes and that researchers can use in future research.

</details>


### [187] [Mechanistic Reaction Prediction via Discrete Flow Matching on Graph-Structured Electron Occupation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27429)
*Nguyen Xuan-Vu,Octavian Susanu,Daniel Armstrong,Philippe Schwaller*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MAELLE是一种基于离散流匹配的化学反应预测模型，将反应建模为电子占据向量上的连续时间马尔可夫链，利用最优传输构造中间编辑轨迹，在USPTO-480K基准上取得竞争力表现，并在分布外场景下保持鲁棒性，同时能恢复与已知化学一致的机理轨迹和预测副产物。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习化学反应建模方法要么通过从头生成产物分子（de novo generation），要么通过启发式图编辑直接操作分子拓扑，两者都未能从本质的电子空间层面建模化学反应。作者希望从"反应本质上是电子空间的变换"这一化学基本原理出发，提出一种更具机理可解释性的反应建模框架。

Method: MAELLE将反应物到产物的映射形式化为定义在所有键合位点、非键合位点和氢位点上的图结构整数值电子占据空间上的连续时间马尔可夫链（CTMC）。通过将离散流匹配混合路径推广到离散电子重排，并利用最优传输（Optimal Transport）构造中间编辑轨迹，生成一系列具有机理可解释性的编辑步骤，无需基础步骤（elementary step）标注。

Result: 在USPTO-480K基准上，MAELLE与主流反应预测模型相比取得具有竞争力的性能。在两种分布外设置（结构复杂度和反应类型）下，MAELLE保持了强性能，而现有方法性能明显下降。此外，MAELLE能恢复与已知化学一致的机理轨迹，并能预测反应的副产物。

Conclusion: 通过在电子占据空间而非分子拓扑层面进行离散流匹配建模，MAELLE不仅取得了有竞争力的反应预测准确率，还展现了更强的分布外鲁棒性和内在的机理可解释性，能够恢复化学反应的真实电子重排轨迹并预测副产物，验证了从电子空间建模化学反应的优越性。

Abstract: Chemical reactions are fundamentally transformations in electron space, yet most machine learning approaches model them either through \textit{de novo} generation of product molecules or through heuristic graph edits that operate directly on molecular topology.
  We introduce MAELLE (\textbf{M}ech\textbf{A}nistic \textbf{E}dit f\textbf{L}ow-matching on e\textbf{L}ectron r\textbf{E}arrangements), which instead models reactions as discrete flow matching over electron occupation vectors.
  Concretely, we formulate the reactant-to-product mapping as a Continuous-time Markov Chain (CTMC) over the graph-structured integer-valued electron occupation space defined on all bonding, non-bonding, and hydrogen sites.
  To construct the intermediate edit trajectories, we generalize the discrete flow matching mixture path to discrete electron rearrangements using Optimal Transport, yielding a sequence of mechanistically interpretable edit moves without requiring elementary step annotations.
  MAELLE achieves competitive performance on the USPTO-480K benchmark compared with leading reaction prediction models.
  Beyond in-distribution accuracy, we evaluate robustness across two out-of-distribution settings - structural complexity and reaction type - and find that MAELLE maintains strong performance where existing methods degrade.
  Finally, because the learned flow operates over the full electron redistribution, MAELLE naturally recovers mechanistic trajectories that align with known chemistry and can predict side products of a reaction.

</details>


### [188] [WikiSkill: Compiling Agent Experience into Persistent Knowledge for Skill Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27454)
*Liyan Tang,Cyrus Rashtchian,Chun-Sung Ferng,Andrew Tomkins,Da-Cheng Juan,Tu Vu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出WikiSkill框架，将智能体技能与持久化知识库（wiki）共同演化，通过分离执行经验、积累知识和可执行技能，持续将经验整合到wiki中供后续技能更新利用，在多个基准和模型上优于现有技能演化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有技能自动发现方法虽然能让智能体通过交互逐步适应，但指导技能开发的经验往往零散分布在优化历史中，缺乏系统性的跨迭代复用机制，限制了技能演化的效率和可迁移性。

Method: WikiSkill框架将原始执行经验、积累的知识和可执行技能三个层次分离：持续将执行经验整合（consolidate）到持久化知识库（wiki）中，后续的技能更新可以基于wiki中积累的知识进行迭代演化，形成一个共同演化的闭环。

Result: 在多样化基准和模型上，WikiSkill一致优于最先进的技能演化方法，并在大多数模型-基准组合下胜过无技能基线。研究发现：技能演化与模型规模互补（大模型受益更多，小模型配技能可超越远大于自己的无技能模型）；演化技能可跨模型及模型家族迁移，且其他模型演化的技能可优于自演化技能；消融实验确认wiki中持久知识积累对有效技能演化至关重要。

Conclusion: 系统性地积累和精炼智能体经验对于开发可复用、可迁移的技能具有显著益处，WikiSkill证明了持久化知识库在技能演化中的关键价值。

Abstract: Agent skills package specialized knowledge and workflows into reusable resources that extend AI agent capabilities. Recent work automatically discovers such skills from agent experience, which enables agents to progressively adapt through interaction. However, the insights that guide skill development typically remain scattered across optimization histories, limiting their systematic reuse across iterations. We introduce WikiSkill, a framework that co-evolves agent skills with a persistent knowledge base (wiki). At a high level, WikiSkill separates raw execution experience, accumulated knowledge, and executable skills, while continuously consolidating experience into the wiki, which subsequent skill updates can build on. Across diverse benchmarks and models, WikiSkill consistently outperforms state-of-the-art skill-evolution methods and improves over no-skill baselines in most model-benchmark settings. We find that skill evolution complements model scaling: larger models generally benefit more from evolved skills, while smaller models with skills can outperform substantially larger models without them. We also find that evolved skills transfer effectively across models and model families, and skills evolved by other models can outperform self-evolved skills. Finally, our ablation studies confirm that persistent knowledge accumulation in the wiki is critical for effective skill evolution. These results demonstrate the benefits of systematically accumulating and refining agent experience for developing reusable and transferable skills.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [189] [Agentic AI Containment Architecture for Security Hardening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26108)
*Mohamed ElBendary*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种Agent遏制架构（Agent Containment Architecture），将多智能体系统的安全性从事后附加层转变为架构级约束属性，通过六种相互作用的设计约束和形式化组织映射，实现可机器验证的合约约束下的Propose-Verify-Act-Verify执行模型，以防御提示注入、编排器操纵、跨会话状态污染和智能体合谋等威胁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体AI系统在自主协调、工具使用和持续学习场景中引入新型安全与治理风险，但现有的遏制方法主要依赖设计后的附加安全层，有时甚至在实现和部署之后才添加，缺乏将安全作为架构内在属性的系统性方法。

Method: 提出Agent Containment Architecture，包含：(1) 从标准系统分析工件到机器可验证合约的形式化组织映射；(2) 六个交互约束：职责分配分离、部署前一致性检查、价值流绑定、时间隔离、积累前严格知识验证、结构与过程完整性的确定性验证；(3) 由这些约束共同强制的Propose-Verify-Act-Verify执行模型；(4) 通过命题和正确性论证将这些约束与关键威胁类防御相关联。

Result: 通过简历筛选案例研究展示该架构在对抗条件下产生可审计、符合策略的结果。论证表明六种约束组合能够防御提示注入、编排器操纵、跨会话状态中毒和突发性智能体合谋等关键威胁类别，同时明确区分结构完整性与语义安全，使残余语义风险显式化且可度量。

Conclusion: 该架构将安全性从后置附加层转变为设计阶段架构约束，通过显式约束界定多智能体系统的设计空间，在保持残余风险可控的同时使残余语义风险透明可见，为多智能体系统安全提供了一种系统化、可验证的架构方案。

Abstract: Multi-agent AI systems are increasingly deployed in contexts where autonomous coordination, tool use, and continuous learning introduce novel security and governance risks. The containment approach to multi-agent system security remains underdeveloped, primarily resorting to add-on layers post-design and sometimes post-implementation and deployment. This paper proposes an Agent Containment Architecture that treats security as an architectural property enforced through a set of explicit constraints that bound the design space of multi-agent systems. The architecture proposed in this paper introduces a novel formal organizational mapping from standard systems analysis artifacts to machine-verifiable contracts under a proposed constraint system. The architecture introduces six interacting constraints: separation of responsibility assignments, pre-deployment coherence checking, value stream binding, temporal isolation, strict knowledge verification before accumulation, and deterministic verification of structural and process integrity. Together, these constraints enforce a Propose-Verify-Act-Verify execution model in which all operations are contractually defined, independently verified, and traceable to specific execution contexts. The paper presents propositions and correctness reasoning arguments linking these constraints to defenses against key threat classes, including prompt injection, orchestrator manipulation, cross-session state poisoning, and emergent agent collusion. A resume screening case study demonstrates how the architecture produces auditable, policy-compliant outcomes under adversarial conditions. The work explicitly separates structural integrity from semantic safety, bounding residual risks while making residual semantic risk explicit and measurable.

</details>


### [190] [Four Ways to Forge a Bundle My Own Verifier Calls Clean: Refusal-Site Mutation Testing of an Evidence-Bundle Verifier](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26183)
*Erik Hill*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 作者构建了一个"陌生人可离线重跑验证"的协议，外部工程师审计发现最廉价的伪造仅需4字节；作者修复后又在自己修复的验证器中找到同类缺陷4次（最廉价的仅1个大写字母）。核心缺陷是"空验证通过"（vacuous pass）：检查沿某路径报告成功却从未检查任何东西。作者不再收集轶事而是做系统测量：在特定提交下验证器暴露112个拒绝点，其中75个可删除而测试仍全绿；自己构建的16个篡改测试只抓到10个，但针对拒绝点本身的测试将覆盖率从0.941提升到1.000。作者的测量工具自身也7次出现同类"空成功"问题。所有数字都是自测自报，论文的信用即在于此。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者构建了一个旨在让陌生人离线重跑验证的协议，但外部审计和后续自查都反复发现同一类缺陷：验证器在"空路径"上报告成功但实际什么也没有检查。作者意识到这不是孤立的轶事而是系统性问题，因此放弃收集个例，转而用可量化的方法系统测量这类"空验证通过"缺陷的分布与严重程度。

Method: 作者首先描述协议设计及外部审计发现的最廉价伪造（4字节）；合并修复后，作者用自己的检测工具在固定验证器上找到同一类缺陷4次（最廉价1个大写字母）。随后放弃轶事转向测量：在特定提交（f59fb62）下按定义的提取规则统计验证器的112个拒绝点，评估删除后测试套件是否仍绿；构建16个篡改测试语料来测试验证器的拒绝能力，又针对拒绝点本身做测试以提升覆盖率；测量工具自身也被纳入检查，发现7次"空成功"。所有测量在作者自建的封闭仓库闭环系统上进行。

Result: 外部审计发现4字节即可伪造；作者自行修复后找到同类缺陷4次。在f59fb62下，112个拒绝点中75个可删除而测试仍全绿（得分0.330）；四个手工发现的伪造中有三个属于存活类别。16个篡改测试只抓到10个（覆盖率0.330左右），但针对性测试拒绝点后将覆盖率从0.941提升到1.000（在146个拒绝点的更大种群上，且序列非单调）。修复四个发现的缺陷只从37/112变为39/119。测量工具自身7次"空成功"，其中4个是为检测此类缺陷而构建的，一个还返回了完美的1.000。

Conclusion: "空验证通过"（vacuous pass）是验证系统的系统性缺陷而非偶发事故：即便经过外部审计和修复，同类问题仍在未审计区域和测量工具自身中反复出现。作者强调所有数据均在自建闭环系统中自我测量，唯一外部数据点是外部审计——这不是论文的缺陷而是其可信度所在。论文透明地披露了测量方法的封闭性局限。

Abstract: I built a protocol whose premise is that a stranger can re-run my claims offline and get the same answer. An outside engineer audited it and broke it: a bundle whose headline numbers were false verified clean, the cheapest forgery four bytes. I merged his fix, then pointed my own instruments at the fixed verifier and found the same defect four more times, in places his audit did not reach. The cheapest is one capital letter.
  The unifying defect is not cryptographic or exotic: a check that reports success along a path where it never examined anything. Vacuous pass is a working label, not a discovery; Section 4 names the literatures already occupying it.
  So I stopped collecting anecdotes and measured. At f59fb62, under the extraction rule of Section 6, the verifier exposes 112 refusal sites; 75 could be deleted with the whole suite and every tamper fixture still green, a score of 0.330. Three of the four hand-found forgeries fall in surviving classes; the fourth is an obligation with no refusal site. Scored alone, the sixteen-fixture corpus built to prove the verifier can refuse catches 10. Testing the refusals themselves took it to 0.941, then to 1.000 at 92e4548 over a grown population of 146 sites; those denominators differ and the series between them is non-monotone, so Section 7.5 carries all eleven, not just the five rows of Table 2. Fixing the four found defects instead moved 37/112 to 39/119, leaving the pre-existing sites at 37.
  Seven times during this study my own measuring tools reported success while measuring nothing; four were built to detect this class, and one returned a perfect 1.000.
  Every number here is self-measured on a system I wrote, over a registry that is a closed loop of my own repositories; the one external data point is the audit of Section 2.2. That is stated here rather than buried: it is the paper's credibility, not a caveat.

</details>


### [191] [Cost-Utility Alignment in LLM Agent Trajectories:Profiling,Attribution,Diagnosis,Adaptation,and Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26195)
*Dan Liu,Jian Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一个以轨迹为中心的"成本-效用对齐"分析框架，将资源消耗与任务贡献视为同一执行过程的双重账本，并围绕五个分析阶段（成本剖析、效用归因、错位诊断、定向适配、评估）组织分析，为LLM智能体的资源感知设计与部署提供结构化基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体通过多步轨迹执行任务，其成本（token、延迟、费用、环境风险）在轨迹中累积，但效用仅在任务聚合层面体现。现有综述要么聚焦推理优化、要么聚焦智能体能力或评估，彼此割裂，缺乏一套原则性工具来判断轨迹资源消耗是否与其任务贡献相匹配，这是本文要填补的空白。

Method: 构建一个轨迹中心的成本-效用对齐框架，将资源消耗与任务贡献作为同一执行的"双重账本"，围绕五个分析阶段展开：成本剖析、效用归因、错位诊断、定向适配与评估。其中效用归因是核心结构，按证据强度对贡献方法分层——从过程代理、信息依赖到反事实重放——为诊断与适配提供因果证据。基于该框架分析近期智能体系统、归因方法与评估协议，覆盖效率、可靠性与经济价值，以及五种错位形式（认知与上下文使用、外部交互、恢复循环控制、资源-能力分配、多智能体协调）及其定向适配。

Result: 形成了一个闭环分析回路，将智能体执行的成本侧与效用侧连接起来，为资源感知的智能体设计与部署提供了结构化基础。框架能系统识别五种错位类型并给出对应的定向适配方案，同时整合了归因方法的证据强度层级用于支撑诊断。

Conclusion: 该框架填补了现有综述将推理优化、智能体能力与评估孤立处理的空白，通过轨迹中心的双账本视角和因果驱动的效用归因，为实践者提供了判断资源支出是否合理的系统性工具，最终支持资源感知的智能体设计与部署。

Abstract: LLM agents execute tasks through multi-step trajectories that accumulate cost in tokens, latency, monetary fees, and environmental risk while producing utility only at the aggregate task level. Prior surveys address inference optimization, agent capabilities, or evaluation in isolation, leaving practitioners without principled tools to determine whether a trajectory's resource expenditure is justified by its task contribution. We address this gap by developing a trajectory-centric cost-utility alignment framework that treats resource consumption and task contribution as dual ledgers over the same execution, organized around five analytical stages: cost profiling, utility attribution, misalignment diagnosis, targeted adaptation, and evaluation. Utility attribution is central to this structure: rather than relying on aggregate outcomes, it organizes contribution methods by evidential strength, from process proxies and information dependency to counterfactual replay, supplying the causal evidence that grounds diagnosis and guides adaptation. Using this framework, we analyze recent agent systems, attribution methods, and evaluation protocols covering efficiency, reliability, and economic value, as well as five forms of misalignment spanning cognitive and context use, external interaction, recovery-loop control, resource-capability allocation, and multi-agent coordination, together with their targeted adaptations. The result is a closed analytical loop connecting the cost side of agent execution to its utility side, providing a structured basis for resource-aware agent design and deployment.

</details>


### [192] [Harness Engineering for Predictable Agentic Systems: An Empirical Study of Deterministic Execution Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26197)
*Saransh Dhage*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究"harness engineering"（将LLM智能体包裹在确定性执行层中：有限状态控制、强制工具选择、输出验证、有界重试和结构化规划）对智能体执行确定性和任务成功率的影响。实验发现初始harness效果不稳定（四模型中仅一个显著提升可复现性，两个显著下降，一个无效果），根因在于自由文本规划步骤成为主要方差来源。通过引入Structured Planning（在调用工具前对计划进行固定schema验证），三/四模型组合达到100%可复现性与确定性，任务成功率在四分之三组合中升至100%，同时token成本全面下降，但延迟呈现模型依赖性的分叉（一个加速、一个显著变慢）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在相同任务和工具下存在显著的运行间执行方差，这在金融、合规等受监管领域不可接受。尽管已有探索性使用能够容忍这种不确定性，但缺乏系统性的"harness engineering"（确定性执行层）方法来研究如何量化并消除该方差，且其代价（延迟、成本）是否模型依赖尚不清楚。

Method: 设计确定性执行层harness，包含五个组件：有限状态控制、强制工具选择、输出验证、有界重试和结构化规划。在两类合成任务（金融、法律）和两个开源模型（Qwen-2.5-7B-Instruct、Gemma-3-27B）上共四个模型-任务组合进行实验。首先运行第一版本harness测量可复现性，再通过trace级诊断定位剩余方差来源，最后引入Structured Planning（在工具调用前使用固定schema验证计划）进行改进，并测量Reproducibility Rate、Determinism Index、任务成功率和token/延迟成本。

Result: 第一版harness结果混合：仅四分之一模型-任务组合显著提升可复现性，两个显著下降，一个无效果。trace级诊断识别出自由文本规划步骤是主要剩余方差源。加入Structured Planning后，三/四组合的Reproducibility Rate和Determinism Index在N=100时达到1.000，任务成功率达100%于四分之三组合。token成本在所有组合中均下降；但延迟呈现模型依赖分叉——一个模型变得更快，另一个明显更慢，且该差异对样本量稳健。

Conclusion: Harness engineering是提升LLM智能体可靠性的一种独立且有效的方法论，但并非"一刀切"方案：其效果与代价（尤其是延迟）具有模型依赖性，必须通过实证测量而非假设来评估。结构化规划是消除执行方差的关键组件，但引入约束的成本需要按模型逐一量化。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) based agents exhibit substantial run-to-run execution variance even when given identical tasks and tools -- acceptable for exploratory use but unacceptable in regulated domains such as finance and compliance. We study harness engineering: wrapping an agent in a deterministic execution layer (finite-state control, forced tool selection, output validation, bounded retry, and structured planning) and measuring its effect on execution determinism and task success. Across two synthetic tasks (finance and legal) and two open-weight models (Qwen-2.5-7B-Instruct, Gemma-3-27B), a first-pass harness produces a mixed result: it significantly improves reproducibility in one of four model-task cells, significantly degrades it in two, and has no effect in the fourth. A trace-level diagnostic finds the cause: once tool sequence, state sequence, and output are already highly consistent, an unconstrained free-text planning step becomes the dominant remaining source of variance. Adding Structured Planning -- validating the plan against a fixed schema before any tool is invoked -- eliminates the effect entirely: three of four cells reach a Reproducibility Rate and Determinism Index of 1.000 at N=100, and task success rises to 100% in three of four cells. The gain is not free: token cost falls in every cell, but latency shows a genuine, sample-size-robust split by model -- one becomes faster under the constraint, the other markedly slower. We argue harness engineering is a distinct, effective discipline for agent reliability, but its cost is model-dependent and must be measured, not assumed.

</details>


### [193] [Fairness Invariants: A Relational Approach to Explaining and Mitigating Fairness Bugs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26209)
*Ranit Debnath Akash,Ashish Kumar,Gang Tan,Saeid Tizpaz-Niari*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: REMI是一个用于自动化定位、解释和缓解个体歧视（个体公平性bug）的框架，将反事实公平性视为关系不变式发现问题，通过双向关系解释学习配对样本，推断可解释的基于规则的"公平性不变式"，作为护栏选择性阻断或重标记不公平预测，无需重训练模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据驱动的软件系统在刑事司法、金融借贷等高风险社会经济领域被广泛部署，但常表现出个体歧视——在仅受保护属性不同的相似个体间产生不公平的结果差异。现有研究主要聚焦于检测和量化这些bug，但缺乏原则性的机制来解释和定位个体公平性bug。现有解释技术多针对单输入决策设计，无法处理歧视本质上涉及的原始样本与反事实样本之间的比较性（关系性）本质。

Method: REMI框架借鉴形式化方法中的循环不变式合成思想，将反事实公平性建模为关系不变式发现问题。具体包括：(1) 提出双向关系解释框架，在配对样本(x, x')上学习以识别公平性被违反的输入空间区域；(2) 与传统的单向蕴含对不同，采用双向约束——要求原始样本和反事实样本产生相同输出；(3) 利用三种数据对齐技术推断可解释的基于规则的模型作为"公平性不变式"；(4) 这些规则作为护栏，可选择性阻断或重标记不公平预测，无需模型重训练。

Result: 在符号程序和神经网络程序上的评估表明，REMI在超过83%的案例中成功定位了ground-truth公平性bug，显著优于最先进的基线方法，并在黑盒模型中最多减少70%的歧视性决策。

Conclusion: REMI提供了一种新颖的、无需重训练模型的个体公平性自动化定位与缓解方案，通过将形式化方法中的不变式合成思想引入公平性分析，有效解决了现有解释技术在关系性歧视场景中的不足，在定位准确率和歧视缓解效果上均显著优于现有方法。

Abstract: Data-driven software systems are increasingly deployed in high-stakes socio-economic domains, from criminal justice to financial lending. However, these systems often exhibit individual discrimination---unjustified disparities in which a program yields different outcomes for similar individuals who differ only in their protected attributes (e.g., race, gender, age). While existing research has focused on detecting and quantifying these bugs, there remains a critical lack of principled mechanisms to explain and localize individual fairness bugs. Current explanation techniques are largely designed for single-input decisions rather than the relational nature of discrimination, which inherently involves a comparison between an original and a counterfactual pair.
  We present REMI, a framework for the automated localization, explanation, and mitigation of individual discrimination. Inspired by loop-invariant synthesis in formal methods, we treat counterfactual fairness as a relational invariant discovery problem. We introduce a bidirectional relational explanation framework that learns over paired examples $(x, x')$ to identify regions of the input space where fairness is violated. Unlike traditional one-way implication pairs used in invariant inference, our approach enforces bidirectional constraints: requiring identical outcomes for both original and counterfactual samples. REMI utilizes three data-alignment techniques to infer interpretable rule-based models that act as "fairness invariants." These rules serve as guardrails to selectively block or relabel unfair predictions without requiring model retraining. Our evaluation on symbolic and neural network programs demonstrates that REMI localizes ground-truth fairness bugs in over 83% of cases, significantly outperforming state-of-the-art baselines and reducing discriminatory decisions in black-box models by up to 70%.

</details>


### [194] [Characterizing the Landscape of Open-Source Satellite Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26211)
*Jinfeng Wen,Qi Liang,Yuehan Sun,Federica Sarro,Ao Zhou,Xuanzhe Liu,Shangguang Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文首次对开源卫星软件生态系统进行系统化实证研究，挖掘并分析了22,286个GitHub卫星相关项目，围绕流行度趋势、软件目标和开发实践三个研究问题展开，构建了涵盖43个软件目标类别的分类体系，并揭示了高度异质化、任务专业化的卫星软件生态系统特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卫星已成为现代技术系统的基础组成部分，支撑通信、导航、地球观测和科学研究等关键基础设施。随着空间探索的推进和卫星服务需求的增长，对复杂、异构卫星软件的依赖不断增加，亟需对卫星软件生态系统进行系统性理解，但现有研究缺乏全面的实证考察。

Method: 本研究通过挖掘和分析22,286个与卫星相关的GitHub项目，从三个研究问题（RQ1流行度趋势、RQ2软件目标、RQ3开发实践）展开分析。具体包括：通过时间演化分析项目与活跃开发者数量趋势；人工检查646个项目构建涵盖概念设计、数据集、系统实现、仿真、测试和工具等六大领域的43个软件目标类别的分类体系；对含源码的项目进行深度分析，考察编程语言多样性和实现策略。

Result: 研究发现卫星开源软件呈现日益增长的流行趋势；构建了43个软件目标类别的分类体系；揭示了使用66种编程语言、高度异质化且任务专业化的卫星软件生态系统。

Conclusion: 该研究总结了关键发现，并为卫星开发者和研究人员提出了可行的实践建议，填补了开源卫星软件实证研究的空白。

Abstract: Satellites have become fundamental components of modern technological systems, supporting critical infrastructure in communication, navigation, Earth observation, and scientific research. As space exploration advances and demand for satellite-enabled services grows, reliance on complex, heterogeneous satellite software continues to increase. A systematic understanding of the satellite software landscape is therefore increasingly important, yet existing studies still lack a comprehensive empirical examination. To address this gap, we present the first characterization study of open-source satellite software, examining its ecosystem and development practices. We mine and analyze 22,286 satellite-related GitHub projects through three research questions on popularity trends (RQ1), software goals (RQ2), and development practices (RQ3). First, we characterize the temporal evolution of projects and active developers, revealing increasing popularity. Second, through manual inspection of 646 projects, we construct a taxonomy of 43 software-goal categories spanning conceptual design, datasets, system implementation, simulation, testing, and tools. Third, we conduct an in-depth analysis of projects with source code, revealing a highly heterogeneous and task-specialized ecosystem with 66 programming languages and diverse implementation strategies. Finally, we summarize key findings and derive actionable implications for satellite developers and researchers.

</details>


### [195] [Challenges and Contributions in Quality of AI-Based Software: A Systematic Mapping Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26215)
*Maryum Hamdani,Mateen Ahmed Abbasi,Marko Jäntti,Markku Tukiainen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过系统映射研究（SMS），综合分析了2020年1月至2026年1月期间来自五个电子数据源的33项主要研究，识别出AI软件质量评估中的六大反复出现的挑战类别，其中最突出的是现有质量评估模型的局限、非功能需求管理问题、质量感知开发和质量保障问题，并提出研究者、工业从业者与标准化组织协作的建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着人工智能日益嵌入现代软件系统，其质量如何定义、评估和保障成为一个重要的开放性问题。本文旨在通过系统映射研究，系统性地梳理AI软件质量领域的现有研究状况，识别关键挑战与研究方向。

Method: 采用系统映射研究（SMS）方法，对2020年1月至2026年1月期间的文献进行系统综述，从五个电子数据源中通过自动化搜索、筛选和滚雪球抽样，最终纳入33项主要研究进行综合分析与分类。

Result: 研究识别出六大反复出现的挑战类别，其中最主要的挑战是现有质量评估模型的局限性，其次是非功能需求管理、质量感知开发以及质量保障等方面的问题。

Conclusion: 研究建议研究者、工业从业者与标准化组织之间应加强合作，以制定全面的质量评估体系及其测量方法，从而更好地应对AI软件质量保障所面临的挑战。

Abstract: Artificial Intelligence (AI) is increasingly embedded in modern software systems, raising important questions about how its quality should be defined, assessed, and assured. This paper presents a Systematic Mapping Study (SMS) on the quality of AI-based software. The study synthesizes primary studies published between January 2020 and January 2026 and selected from five electronic data sources. A total of 33 primary studies were included after automated search, screening, and snowballing. The results identify six recurring challenge categories, with the most prominent being limitations in existing quality assessment models, followed by issues in non-functional requirement management, quality-aware development, and quality assurance. The findings suggest a call for collaboration of researchers and industrial practitioners with standardization organizations, that could possibly devise comprehensive quality assessments and their measurement methods.

</details>


### [196] [The Green Software Landscape: A Systematic Mapping Study on Evolution, Applications, Software Lifecycle, and Best Practices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26229)
*Max Hort,Maria Kechagia,Federica Sarro*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对2010至2024年间发表的390篇绿色软件工程（Green SE）研究进行了系统映射研究，按应用领域和研究类型分类，并深入分析79篇代表性论文中能量测量实验的关键要素，发现SE会议承载了大部分能量相关文献，2023年起受AI相关研究驱动数量激增，优化与基准测试是最主流的研究类型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 能源消耗和气候变化使可持续性成为软件工程领域的关键议题，催生了绿色软件工程（Green SE）这一研究方向。过去15年间该领域积累了丰富的研究成果，但缺乏对其演变趋势的系统性梳理，因此需要通过系统映射研究来全面了解该领域的发展脉络、研究热点和实验实践现状。

Method: 采用系统映射研究方法，收集2010至2024年间390篇绿色软件工程出版物，按照应用领域（如移动、云、AI）和研究类型（如优化研究、基准测试、文献综述）进行分类；同时从390篇中选取79篇代表性论文，深入分析其能量测量实验中涉及的关键要素，包括硬件、测量方法、稳定性和可复现性。

Result: 研究发现软件工程会议发表了大部分能量相关文献；绿色软件工程研究自2023年起出现显著增长，主要由AI相关研究推动；优化研究和基准测试是最普遍的研究类型；通过79篇代表性论文的深入分析，揭示了能量测量实验中对硬件、测量、稳定性和可复现性等关键要素的考虑情况。

Conclusion: 该研究旨在告知SE社区当前的能源问题应对方法，强调关键的实验实践规范，并倡导继续推动更可持续的软件工程项目。

Abstract: Energy consumption and climate change have made sustainability critical in Software Engineering (SE), driving the emergence of Green SE. Over the past 15 years, numerous solutions for sustainable software systems have been published by the SE community, offering a rich resource for analyzing the field's evolution. To explore this, we conducted a systematic mapping study of Green SE research published between 2010 and 2024. We collected 390 publications, categorizing them by application domain (e.g., mobile, cloud, AI) and research type (e.g., optimisation study, benchmarking, literature review Additionally, we analyzed a representative subset of 79 papers to classify the key elements-such as hardware, measurement, stability, and replicability-considered during energy measurement experiments. Our findings indicate that SE conferences host the majority of energy-related literature. Notably, Green SE studies surged in popularity starting in 2023, largely driven by AI-related publications. Optimization and benchmarking emerged as the most prevalent research types. Ultimately, we aim to inform the SE community about current approaches to energy concerns, highlight critical experimental practices, and advocate for continued action toward more sustainable software engineering.

</details>


### [197] ["A Second Set of Eyes": The Process and Challenges of Software Documentation Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26232)
*Avinash Bhat,Ian Arawjo,Disha Shrivastava,Jin L. C. Guo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究通过对31位资深技术写作者的半结构化访谈，揭示了文档评审过程中五个不同阶段（自审、技术评审、编辑评审、试用测试和发布后反馈），并阐明了保持文档质量所需的个人和协作努力，以及作者在招募评审专家、协调开发时间线和使用非专用工具时所面临的组织与技术挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究定义了"好"文档的质量标准，但尚未考察实践者如何运用这些专业知识来改进文档质量，以及他们在这一过程中面临的具体挑战。文档生产所需的知识分散在开发者、管理者和其他实践者之间，这种分布式知识背景下的协作评审过程尚未被系统研究。

Method: 采用半结构化访谈方法，对来自不同组织的31位经验丰富的技术写作者进行深入访谈，通过质性分析提炼文档评审过程中的关键阶段、参与者角色、专业分工以及组织与技术层面的障碍。

Result: 识别出文档评审的五个不同阶段：自审、技术评审、编辑评审、试用测试和发布后反馈。每个阶段依赖于具有不同专业知识的实践者来分别解决内容、呈现和用户体验维度的质量问题。同时发现了写作者在招募评审专家、应对开发时间线约束以及使用非专门为文档工作流设计的工具时所面临的组织和技术挑战。

Conclusion: 文档评审是一个关键但尚未被充分研究的协作工作场所，本研究为其开辟了流程改进和工具支持的新的研究及设计方向。

Abstract: Organizations assign documentation work to technical writers, yet the knowledge required to produce it is distributed across developers, managers, and other practitioners. Prior work has established quality criteria for judging "good" documentation, but it has not examined how practitioners bring that expertise to improve documentation quality or the challenges they face in doing so. Through semi-structured interviews with experienced technical writers ($n=31$) from different organizations, our work reveals the individual and collaborative effort required to maintain documentation quality. We identify five distinct stages of the documentation review process: self review, technical review, editorial review, play testing, and post-publication feedback. Each stage draws on practitioners with distinct expertise to address quality across content, presentation, and user experience. Our findings surface organizational and technical challenges writers face in recruiting expert reviewers, navigating development timelines, and contending with tools not specifically designed for documentation workflows. Our work positions documentation review as a crucial yet understudied site of collaborative work and opens new research and design directions for process improvement and tool support.

</details>


### [198] [When Review Alone No Longer Scales: Layered Supervision in AI-Assisted Software Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26316)
*Markus Stolze,Mirco Strässle*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过五名软件工程从业者的定性访谈研究，探讨AI辅助开发工具对现有质量保障机制（guardrails）的影响，发现组织正从以代码评审为中心的模式转向分层监督（layered supervision），包括预防性护栏、可执行护栏和人工监督三层协同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI辅助开发工具使软件工程师能以更高速度和更大规模生成代码实现，这给传统的质量保障护栏（如代码评审、linting、测试和CI/CD流水线）带来了巨大压力，但关于这些护栏如何演化以适应这一变化的研究非常有限。

Method: 采用质性访谈研究方法，对五名软件工程实践者进行了访谈研究，该研究置于更广泛的从业者调查背景之中。

Result: 研究发现组织将监督工作分布在多个护栏层：预防性护栏（将架构意图和约定外化为机器可解释形式）、可执行护栏（将linting、测试和CI/CD重新定位为可扩展的监督基础设施）、以及人工监督（从逐行检查转向以架构推理、可解释性和长期可维护性为重点的监督性解读）。

Conclusion: 研究将其特征描述为从以评审为中心的护栏向分层监督的转变，在此模式下没有任何单一护栏能够独立承担全部监督负担。

Abstract: AI-assisted development tools enable software engineers to generate implementations at substantially higher speed and volume than in traditional workflows. Software teams have long relied on guardrails -- standing control mechanisms such as code review, linting, testing, and CI/CD pipelines -- to maintain quality and coordination. High-throughput AI-assisted generation increases pressure on these guardrails -- straining their capacity to keep pace with the volume and rate of generated changes -- and reshapes how organizations supervise development workflows, yet relatively little is known about how existing guardrails evolve in response. We conducted a qualitative interview study with five software engineering practitioners, situated within a broader practitioner survey. Our findings indicate that organizations distribute the work of supervision across multiple guardrail layers: preventive guardrails (produced by externalizing architectural intent and conventions into machine-interpretable form), executable guardrails (linting, testing, and CI/CD repurposed as scalable supervision infrastructure), and human oversight (shifting from line-by-line inspection toward supervisory interpretation focused on architectural reasoning, explainability, and long-term maintainability). We characterize this as a transition from review- centric guardrails toward layered supervision, in which no single guardrail carries the supervision load alone.

</details>


### [199] [Investigating Software Aging in LLM-Generated Software Systems across Generation-and-Execution Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26391)
*Cesar Santos,Michele Vitagliano,Roberto Natella,Ermeson Andrade*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究实验性地调查了LLM生成的服务型应用在不同编程语言下的软件老化症状。使用BaxBench派生的后端场景，生成了JavaScript、Python和Rust应用，经过48小时负载执行后监测内存使用、响应时间和吞吐量，并用Mann-Kendall检验和Sen斜率估计器进行分析，辅以静态代码分析和与人类编写实现的人工对比。结果显示内存使用是最一致的软件老化指标，大多数应用-语言组合呈现显著上升趋势，而功能正确性不足以评估LLM生成软件的运行可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已有研究考察了LLM生成代码的功能正确性、安全性、可维护性和鲁棒性，但对LLM生成的软件系统在持续运行下的长期可靠性（即软件老化）知之甚少。部署前的功能正确性验证无法保证系统在连续运行环境中的运行可靠性，因此需要实验性调查LLM生成软件的软件老化症状。

Method: 1) 从BaxBench获取后端场景，通过LLM生成平台生成JavaScript、Python和Rust三种语言的应用；2) 使用BaxBench派生的测试进行验证；3) 进行48小时负载运行，监测内存使用、响应时间和吞吐量；4) 使用Mann-Kendall检验和Sen斜率估计器进行趋势分析；5) 对生成代码进行静态分析，识别代码级老化机制；6) 与人类编写的相关后端场景实现进行探索性对比。

Result: 内存使用是最一致的软件老化潜在指标，大多数应用-语言组合呈现统计显著的上升趋势；响应时间和吞吐量表现出更异质的行为。静态分析识别出了合理的代码级老化机制，且人类编写的系统同样可能出现老化趋势。

Conclusion: 功能正确性不足以评估LLM生成的软件在持续运行环境中的部署前操作可靠性。需要额外的可靠性评估指标和方法来确保LLM生成软件在长期运行中的稳定性。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used to generate executable software systems from natural language specifications, accelerating development and reducing manual implementation effort. Although recent studies have investigated the functional correctness, security, maintainability, and robustness of LLM-generated code, little is known about the long-term reliability of such systems under sustained execution. In this paper, we experimentally investigate software aging symptoms in LLM-generated service-based applications across different programming languages. Using backend scenarios derived from BaxBench, we generated applications targeting JavaScript, Python, and Rust through LLM-based generation platforms, validated them with BaxBench-derived tests, and subjected them to 48-hour workload executions. We monitored memory usage, response time, and throughput and analyzed them using the Mann--Kendall test and Sen's slope estimator. We further complemented the runtime evaluation with static analysis of the generated source code and an exploratory comparison with human-written implementations of related backend scenarios. The results show that memory usage is the most consistent indicator of potential software aging, with statistically significant upward trends in most application-language combinations, while response time and throughput exhibit more heterogeneous behavior. Static analysis identified plausible code-level aging mechanisms, and the comparison with human-written systems showed that aging trends can also emerge in manually developed implementations. These findings indicate that functional correctness alone is insufficient to assess the operational reliability of LLM-generated software before deployment in continuously running environments.

</details>


### [200] [Spec2Vision: Contract-Guided Delivery of AI-Generated Computer Vision Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26400)
*Ghfran Jabour,Sergey Ivanov*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Spec2Vision是一个实验框架，通过分阶段运行时使任务合约在合成、筛选、测试和有界修复中保持显式，从而弥合生成的计算机视觉代码"可运行"与"满足下游评估器任务合约"之间的差距。在850次主评估中达到81/85的评估通过率，显著优于仅依赖更丰富任务规格提示的基础模型（最高35/85）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成的计算机视觉代码即使可以运行，也可能不满足下游评估器强制要求的任务合约。这种"可运行但未交付"的差距是现有代码生成方法忽视的关键问题，需要系统性研究。

Method: 提出Spec2Vision实验框架，通过分阶段运行时（staged runtime）将任务合约在整个流程中显式化，包括：合成（synthesis）、筛选（screening）、测试（testing）和有界修复（bounded repair）。此外还包含了结构性修复、兼容性脚手架和生成器预检等多个关键组件，并通过消融实验验证各组件贡献。

Result: 主评估中81/85通过；移除结构性修复降至55/85，移除兼容性脚手架降至58/85，移除生成器预检降至39/85。单智能体基线（从轻量任务接地到直接源码规格暴露）性能明显更弱（17/85至35/85）。轻量级基线85/85可运行但仅17/85通过评估、6/85严格交付成功。

Conclusion: 可运行性不等于交付成功；最强的证据表明，保持任务合约在分阶段生成、检查和修复过程中显式化是提升规格遵守能力的关键。项目提供了工件以支持审计和复现。

Abstract: Generated computer-vision code can be runnable without satisfying the task contract enforced by a downstream evaluator. We study that gap with Spec2Vision, an experimental framework for producing and evaluating specification-grounded CV pipeline bundles through a staged runtime that keeps the task contract explicit across synthesis, screening, testing, and bounded repair. The benchmark evaluates 17 CV tasks, 10 executable conditions, and 5 repeats per task-condition cell, for 850 primary runs. In the primary 850-run evaluation, Spec2Vision reaches 81/85 evaluator-test passes; removing structural repair drops to 55/85, compatibility scaffolding to 58/85, and generator preflight to 39/85. The executable single-agent baselines expose progressively richer task specifications to the model, culminating in direct source-spec exposure, yet remain much weaker overall, from 17/85 for lightweight task grounding to 35/85 evaluator-test passes. The lightweight baseline nevertheless remains core-runnable in 85/85 runs but reaches only 17/85 evaluator-test passes and 6/85 strict-delivery successes, showing that runnability is not equivalent to delivery. Across this benchmark, the strongest evidence comes from keeping the task contract explicit across staged generation, checking, and repair. Artifacts are provided to support audit of run bundles, model-visible inputs, and derived tables.

</details>


### [201] [STILL: Recovering Lowered STL Semantics for LLM-assisted C++ Decompilation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26408)
*Xiaohan Wang,Kevin Leach*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出STILL，一个结构化的语义接口，从剥离符号后的控制流图中预测函数级的STL容器语义，并以紧凑提示的形式为LLM反编译优化提供指导。在StlBench上验证了预测能力，在stripped HumanEval反编译任务中，借助这些提示使DeepSeek-chat的可执行性从17.4%（无提示）提升至28.4%，远超Ghidra原始反编译的8.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM辅助反编译在可读性和可重执行性上有所提升，但在处理剥离符号的C++函数（尤其涉及标准模板库STL时）仍表现不佳。编译、优化和符号剥离会移除或模糊源码级语义（如容器类型和库调用结构），而传统反编译器输出往往无法恢复这些语义，导致反编译质量受限。

Method: STILL通过一个结构化语义接口，从剥离符号的控制流图中预测函数级别的STL容器语义，并将这些语义渲染为紧凑的提示，供LLM在反编译精化（refinement）过程中使用。

Result: 在StlBench上，STILL对常见容器级STL语义的预测表现良好，在跨数据集上对stable string和vector切片的结果最为稳健。在stripped HumanEval反编译任务中，STILL提示使DeepSeek-chat精化达到28.4%的可执行性，对比无提示精化的17.4%和Ghidra原始反编译的8.9%。提示效用取决于下游backbone，反编译专用模型需要轻量适配才能从同一接口受益。

Conclusion: STILL证明从剥离的控制流图中恢复STL容器语义并将其作为LLM提示是可行的，能显著提升反编译代码的可执行性；但提示的收益并非背骨无关，需针对不同反编译模型进行相应适配。

Abstract: LLM-assisted decompilation improves readability and re-executability, but still underperforms on stripped C++ functions that use the Standard Template Library (STL). Compilation, optimization, and symbol stripping remove or obscure source-level semantics such as container types and library-call structure, while traditional decompiler output often fails to recover them. We present STILL, a structured semantic interface that predicts function-level STL container semantics from stripped control-flow graphs and renders them as compact hints for LLM refinement. On StlBench, STILL predicts common container-level STL semantics, with the strongest cross-dataset results for stable string and vector slices. On stripped HumanEval decompilation, these hints enable DeepSeek-chat refinement to reach 28.4% executability, compared with 17.4% for no-hint refinement and 8.9% for raw Ghidra decompilation; hint utility is downstream-backbone-dependent, with decompilation-specialized models requiring lightweight adaptation to benefit from the same interface.

</details>


### [202] [DeepRepro: State-Aware Subplanning for Paper-to-Code Reproduction in Evolving Repositories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26557)
*Hongru Song,Ruqing Zhang,Jiafeng Guo,Xueqi Cheng,Maarten de Rijke*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: DeepRepro是一个基于执行状态感知的子规划框架，用于ML论文到代码的自动复现。它通过动态将仓库状态和运行时反馈转化为细粒度的实现子计划，使规划与执行始终保持对齐，在PaperBench Code-Dev上优于多种强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有agentic LLM在论文到代码复现任务上表现不佳，传统系统依赖静态的前期规划和顺序的逐文件生成，导致当依赖关系、接口和运行反馈随时间变化时产生不一致性。该任务本质上是长时程问题，需要构建和维护一个在执行过程中状态持续演变的完整功能化仓库，现有方法无法应对这种动态复杂性。

Method: DeepRepro提出状态感知框架，核心组件包括：(1) 执行状态感知的子规划机制，将演变的仓库状态和运行时反馈动态转化为细粒度的实现子计划；(2) 仓库感知的编排策略，确保子任务间的协调一致；(3) 轻量级进程感知接口，用于长时程复现过程的透明监控。

Result: 在PaperBench Code-Dev基准上的实验表明，DeepRepro稳定优于多种强科学和商业代码智能体基线。

Conclusion: DeepRepro验证了在长时程论文复现任务中，让规划与执行动态对齐（而非静态一次性规划）的有效性，为ML论文到代码的自动复现提供了一种更可靠的技术路径。

Abstract: Recent advances in agentic large language models (LLMs) have enabled increasingly autonomous software engineering workflows, yet automatic machine learning (ML) paper-to-code reproduction remains a challenging long-horizon problem. Unlike conventional code generation, this task requires constructing and maintaining a fully functional repository whose state continuously evolves during execution. Existing systems typically rely on static upfront planning followed by sequential file-level generation, which often leads to inconsistencies as dependencies, interfaces, and execution feedback change over time. We propose DeepRepro, a state-aware framework for paper-to-code reproduction based on execution-state-aware subplanning. DeepRepro dynamically transforms evolving repository states and runtime feedback into fine-grained implementation subplans, keeping planning aligned with execution throughout repository construction. The framework further incorporates repository-aware orchestration and a lightweight process-aware interface for transparent monitoring of long-horizon reproduction. Experiments on PaperBench Code-Dev show that DeepRepro consistently outperforms strong scientific and commercial code-agent baselines.

</details>


### [203] [The Thousand-Graph Hypothesis: A Testable Hypothesis of Task-Conditioned Relation Materialization in Repository-Level Code Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26602)
*Fei Ding*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种仅实体（entity-only）的外部接口方案，在推理阶段通过任务条件化关系物化（task-conditioned relation materialization）来解决大型软件仓库超出模型上下文限制的问题。采用双层索引（全局路由+局部实体聚焦），在DeepSeek-V4-Flash和SWE-bench Verified上验证，零预构建实体关系边的条件下达到95.6%的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型软件仓库往往超出模型上下文限制；将仓库知识训练进模型成本高且容易过时；本地检索可能遗漏分散的需求；显式关系图谱需要持续的维护负担。现有方法难以兼顾效率、准确性和可维护性。

Method: 提出entity-only外部接口，通过任务条件化关系物化在推理阶段动态构建实体间关系；采用两层索引结构：第一层进行全局路由（global routing），第二层实现局部实体聚焦（local entity focus）。在零预构建entity-relation边的条件下评估。

Result: 在SWE-bench Verified上使用DeepSeek-V4-Flash评估：base条件（基线）成功率92.1%，一层索引94.2%，两层索引95.6%。两层索引相比基线提升了3.5个百分点。

Conclusion: 实体唯一的外部接口结合推理时任务条件化关系物化与双层索引，能有效处理超出上下文限制的大型软件仓库，无需预构建关系图谱即可获得显著性能提升，兼顾了低维护成本和高检索准确度。

Abstract: Large software repositories are often beyond model context limits. Training repository knowledge into models is costly and quickly stale, while local retrieval can miss scattered requirements, and explicit relation graphs add ongoing maintenance burden. We propose an entity-only external interface with task-conditioned relation materialization during inference. A two-layer index separates global routing from local entity focus and is evaluated on DeepSeek-V4-Flash and SWE-bench Verified. The base, one-layer, and two-layer conditions achieve 92.1%, 94.2%, and 95.6% success, respectively, under zero pre-built entity-relation edges.

</details>


### [204] [Processing/p5 Defined through Practice and Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26614)
*Kit Kuksenok,Lee Tusman*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文基于在JavaScript和Lua两种宿主语言中实现Processing/p5的案例研究，提出了构成Processing/p5创意编程框架的软件决策指导要素清单，并讨论了这些要素在其他探索性和创造性工具中的不同实现方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Processing/p5库在不同编程语言中展现出一致的创意编码优先级，但不同语言生态各有其独特的affordances、社区规范和用法模式。作者希望通过跨语言案例研究，提炼出构成Processing/p5本质的软件决策指导方面，以促进学习、研究和艺术实践。

Method: 通过两个案例研究——分别在JavaScript和Lua两种宿主语言中构建Processing/p5的实现——进行对比分析，归纳出跨宿主语言不变的软件决策指导要素清单，并在其他探索性和创造性工具中考察这些要素的不同操作化方式。

Result: 提出了一个构成Processing/p5的软件决策制定指导方面清单，这些方面在宿主语言间保持一致。该框架在其他创造性工具中也得到了验证，展示了每个指导方面可以被不同地操作化。

Conclusion: 该清单为创建新的Processing/p5库提供了指导，为创意编码和算法艺术领域的学习、研究和艺术创作开辟了新的机会。

Abstract: Processing/p5 libraries across different programming languages enact consistent priorities for creative coding as a designed experience. While different programming language ecosystems, like Java and JavaScript, are each associated with their own affordances, community norms, and patterns of use, Processing/p5 sketches across these languages share similarities. Based on case studies of building an implementation of Processing/p5 in two host languages, JavaScript and Lua, we propose a list of software decision-making guiding aspects that constitute Processing/p5, regardless of host language. We discuss this framework in the context of decisions in other exploratory and creative tools that demonstrate how each of the guiding aspects can be operationalized differently than in the case studies. The proposed list highlights opportunities for learning, research, and artistic practice through creation of new Processing/p5 libraries for creative coding and algorithmic art.

</details>


### [205] [FaultLens: Learning Compact Behavioral Test Suites for Generated Operational Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26746)
*Zeming Liu,Hang Lyu,Jingtao Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: FaultLens是一种从已执行证据中学习紧凑行为测试套件的方法，通过共享的故障-探针杀死关系缓存和混合贪心（故障驱动+多样性驱动）排序策略，仅用穷举域的1.2-2.0%即覆盖99.0%的动态可杀死故障，并在下游部署中消除严重尾部回归。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
